   25: function formatDate(value: Date | null): string {
   26:   if (!value) return "No due date";
   27: 
   28:   return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", {
   29:     day: "2-digit",

   35: export default function ProgrammingAssignmentPage() {
   36:   const params = useParams<{ assignmentId: string }>();
   37:   const { user } = useAuth();
   38: 
   39:   const assignmentId = params.assignmentId;
   40: 
   41:   const [assignment, setAssignment] =
   42:     useState<ProgrammingAssignment | null>(null);
   43: 
   44:   const [submission, setSubmission] =
   45:     useState<ProgrammingSubmission | null>(null);
   46: 
   47:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   48:   const [error, setError] = useState("");
   49: 
   50:   useEffect(() => {
   51:     const studentId = user?.uid;
   52:     const currentAssignmentId = assignmentId;
   53: 
   54:     if (!studentId || !currentAssignmentId) {
   55:       return;
   56:     }
   57: 
   58:     let cancelled = false;
   59: 
   60:     async function loadAssignment(
   61:       safeStudentId: string,
   62:       safeAssignmentId: string,
   63:     ) {
   64:       try {
   65:         setLoading(true);
   66:         setError("");
   67: 
   68:         const loaded =
   69:           await getProgrammingAssignmentById(
   70:             safeAssignmentId,
   71:           );
   72: 
   73:         if (cancelled) return;
   74: 
   75:         if (!loaded) {
   76:           setError(
   77:             "This programming assignment could not be found.",
   78:           );
   79:           return;
   80:         }
   81: 
   82:         if (
   83:           !loaded.studentIds.includes(
   84:             safeStudentId,
   85:           )
   86:         ) {
   87:           setError(
   88:             "You are not enrolled in this programming assignment.",
   89:           );
   90:           return;
   91:         }
   92: 
   93:         setAssignment(loaded);
   94: 
   95:         await startStudentAssignment(
   96:           loaded.id,
   97:           safeStudentId,
   98:         );
   99: 
  100:         const loadedSubmission =
  101:           await getProgrammingSubmission(
  102:             loaded.id,
  103:             safeStudentId,
  104:           );
  105: 
  106:         if (!cancelled) {
  107:           setSubmission(
  108:             loadedSubmission,
  109:           );
  110:         }
  111:       } catch (caughtError) {
  112:         if (!cancelled) {
  113:           setError(
  114:             caughtError instanceof Error
  115:               ? caughtError.message
  116:               : "The programming assignment could not be loaded.",
  117:           );
  118:         }
  119:       } finally {
  120:         if (!cancelled) {
  121:           setLoading(false);
  122:         }
  123:       }
  124:     }
  125: 
  126:     void loadAssignment(
  127:       studentId,
  128:       currentAssignmentId,
  129:     );
  130: 
  131:     return () => {
  132:       cancelled = true;
  133:     };
  134:   }, [assignmentId, user?.uid]);
  135: 
  136:   if (loading) {
  137:     return (
  138:       <main className="flex min-h-[70vh] items-center justify-center">
  139:         <div className="text-center">
  140:           <Loader2 className="mx-auto h-10 w-10 animate-spin text-cyan-700" />
  141: 
  142:           <p className="mt-4 font-bold text-slate-600">
  143:             Loading programming assignment...
  144:           </p>
  145:         </div>
  146:       </main>
  147:     );
  148:   }
  149: 
  150:   if (error || !assignment) {
  151:     return (
  152:       <main className="mx-auto max-w-3xl py-10">
  153:         <section className="rounded-3xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-8">
  154:           <h1 className="text-2xl font-black text-red-950">
  155:             Programming assignment unavailable
  156:           </h1>
  157: 
  158:           <p className="mt-3 text-red-800">
  159:             {error ||
  160:               "The assignment could not be loaded."}
  161:           </p>
  162: 
  163:           <Link
  164:             href="/assignments"
  165:             className="mt-6 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-red-700 px-5 py-3 font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-red-800"
  166:           >
  167:             <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
  168:             Back to assignments
  169:           </Link>
  170:         </section>
  171:       </main>
  172:     );
  173:   }
  174: 
  175:   const completed =
  176:     submission?.status === "completed";
  177: 
  178:   return (
  179:     <div className="space-y-6">
  180:       <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
  181:         <Link
  182:           href="/assignments"
  183:           className="inline-flex w-fit items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 transition hover:bg-slate-50"
  184:         >
  185:           <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
  186:           Back to assignments
  187:         </Link>
  188: 
  189:         <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-500">
  190:           {completed ? (
  191:             <>
  192:               <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4 text-emerald-600" />
  193:               Assignment completed
  194:             </>
  195:           ) : (
  196:             <>
  197:               <Clock3 className="h-4 w-4 text-amber-600" />
  198:               Passing all tests completes this assignment automatically
  199:             </>
  200:           )}
  201:         </div>
  202:       </div>
  203:

  205:         <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 lg:flex-row lg:items-end lg:justify-between">

  208:               Programming assignment

   72: function getStatusLabel(
   73:   assignment: UnifiedAssignment,

  167: function matchesFilter(
  168:   assignment: UnifiedAssignment,
  169:   filter: AssignmentFilter,
  170: ): boolean {
  171:   if (filter === "all") return true;
  172:   if (filter === "overdue") {
  173:     return isUnifiedAssignmentOverdue(
  174:       assignment,
  175:     );
  176:   }
  177:   if (filter === "resources") {
  178:     return (
  179:       assignment.kind === "resource"
  180:     );
  181:   }
  182:   if (filter === "quizzes") {
  183:     return assignment.kind === "quiz";
  184:   }
  185:   if (filter === "exams") {
  186:     return assignment.kind === "exam";
  187:   }
  188:   if (filter === "programming") {
  189:     return (
  190:       assignment.kind ===
  191:       "programming"
  192:     );
  193:   }
  194:   if (filter === "completed") {
  195:     return isUnifiedAssignmentComplete(
  196:       assignment,
  197:     );
  198:   }
  199:   if (filter === "submitted") {
  200:     return (
  201:       assignment.kind === "exam" &&
  202:       (assignment.status ===
  203:         "submitted" ||
  204:         assignment.status ===
  205:           "marking")
  206:     );
  207:   }
  208: 
  209:   return (
  210:     assignment.status === filter
  211:   );
  212: }
  213: 
  214: function getHref(
  215:   assignment: UnifiedAssignment,
  216: ): string {
  217:   if (
  218:     assignment.kind ===
  219:     "programming"
  220:   ) {
  221:     return `/assignments/programming/${assignment.id}`;
  222:   }
  223: 
  224:   if (assignment.kind === "quiz") {
  225:     return `/quiz?topic=${encodeURIComponent(
  226:       assignment.resourceId,
  227:     )}&assignment=${encodeURIComponent(
  228:       assignment.id,
  229:     )}`;
  230:   }
  231: 
  232:   if (assignment.kind === "exam") {
  233:     return `/assignments/exam/${assignment.id}`;
  234:   }
  235: 
  236:   return `/assignments/${assignment.id}`;
  237: }

  239: function getActionLabel(
  240:   assignment: UnifiedAssignment,
  241: ): string {
  242:   if (

  244:     "programming"

  290:

  293:     "in_progress"

  371: export default function StudentAssignmentsPage() {
  372:   const { user } = useAuth();
  373: 
  374:   const [assignments, setAssignments] =
  375:     useState<UnifiedAssignment[]>([]);
  376:   const [loading, setLoading] =
  377:     useState(true);
  378:   const [error, setError] =
  379:     useState("");
  380:   const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] =
  381:     useState("");
  382:   const [filter, setFilter] =
  383:     useState<AssignmentFilter>("all");
  384: 
  385:   const loadAssignments =
  386:     useCallback(async () => {
  387:       await Promise.resolve();
  388: 
  389:       if (!user?.uid) {
  390:         setAssignments([]);
  391:         setLoading(false);
  392:         return;
  393:       }
  394: 
  395:       try {
  396:         setLoading(true);
  397:         setError("");
  398: 
  399:         setAssignments(
  400:           await getUnifiedStudentAssignments(
  401:             user.uid,
  402:           ),
  403:         );
  404:       } catch (loadError) {
  405:         console.error(
  406:           "Unable to load student assignments:",
  407:           loadError,
  408:         );
  409: 
  410:         setAssignments([]);
  411: 
  412:         setError(
  413:           loadError instanceof Error
  414:             ? loadError.message
  415:             : "Your assignments could not be loaded.",
  416:         );
  417:       } finally {
  418:         setLoading(false);
  419:       }
  420:     }, [user]);
  421: 
  422:   useEffect(() => {
  423:     void Promise.resolve().then(() => loadAssignments());
  424:   }, [loadAssignments]);
  425: 
  426:   const filteredAssignments =
  427:     useMemo(() => {
  428:       const search =
  429:         searchTerm
  430:           .trim()
  431:           .toLowerCase();
  432: 
  433:       return assignments.filter(
  434:         (assignment) => {
  435:           const matchesSearch =
  436:             !search ||
  437:             assignment.title
  438:               .toLowerCase()
  439:               .includes(search) ||
  440:             assignment.topic
  441:               .toLowerCase()
  442:               .includes(search) ||
  443:             assignment.className
  444:               .toLowerCase()
  445:               .includes(search) ||
  446:             assignment.teacherName
  447:               .toLowerCase()
  448:               .includes(search) ||
  449:             assignment.resourceType
  450:               .toLowerCase()
  451:               .includes(search);
  452: 
  453:           return (
  454:             matchesFilter(
  455:               assignment,
  456:               filter,
  457:             ) && matchesSearch
  458:           );
  459:         },
  460:       );
  461:     }, [
  462:       assignments,
  463:       filter,
  464:       searchTerm,
  465:     ]);
  466: 
  467:   const summary = useMemo(
  468:     () => ({
  469:       total: assignments.length,
  470:       resources: assignments.filter(
  471:         (item) =>
  472:           item.kind === "resource",
  473:       ).length,
  474:       quizzes: assignments.filter(
  475:         (item) =>
  476:           item.kind === "quiz",
  477:       ).length,
  478:       exams: assignments.filter(
  479:         (item) =>
  480:           item.kind === "exam",
  481:       ).length,
  482:       programming:
  483:         assignments.filter(
  484:           (item) =>
  485:             item.kind ===
  486:             "programming",
  487:         ).length,
  488:       notStarted:
  489:         assignments.filter(
  490:           (item) =>
  491:             item.status ===
  492:             "not_started",
  493:         ).length,
  494:       inProgress:
  495:         assignments.filter(
  496:           (item) =>
  497:             item.status ===
  498:             "in_progress",
  499:         ).length,
  500:       submitted:
  501:         assignments.filter(
  502:           (item) =>
  503:             item.kind === "exam" &&
  504:             (item.status ===
  505:               "submitted" ||
  506:               item.status ===
  507:                 "marking"),
  508:         ).length,
  509:       completed:
  510:         assignments.filter(
  511:           isUnifiedAssignmentComplete,
  512:         ).length,
  513:       overdue:
  514:         assignments.filter(
  515:           isUnifiedAssignmentOverdue,
  516:         ).length,
  517:     }),
  518:     [assignments],
  519:   );
  520: 
  521:   if (loading) {
  522:     return (
  523:       <main className="flex min-h-[70vh] items-center justify-center px-6 py-10">
  524:         <div className="text-center">
  525:           <Loader2 className="mx-auto h-10 w-10 animate-spin text-blue-600" />
  526: 
  527:           <p className="mt-4 font-semibold text-slate-600">
  528:             Loading your assignments...
  529:           </p>
  530:         </div>
  531:       </main>
  532:     );
  533:   }
  534: 
  535:   return (
  536:     <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-7xl space-y-8 px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
  537:       <section className="overflow-hidden rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-700 via-indigo-700 to-violet-700 p-7 text-white shadow-xl sm:p-10">
  538:         <p className="text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-blue-100">
  539:           Student workspace
  540:         </p>
  541: 
  542:         <h1 className="mt-3 text-3xl font-black sm:text-4xl">
  543:           My Assignments
  544:         </h1>
  545: 
  546:         <p className="mt-3 max-w-2xl text-blue-100">
  547:           Complete resources, quizzes,
  548:           programming challenges and written
  549:           assessments, monitor due dates and
  550:           review your results.
  551:         </p>
  552:       </section>
  553: 
  554:       {error && (
  555:         <section className="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-5 text-red-800">
  556:           <AlertCircle className="mt-0.5 h-5 w-5 shrink-0" />
  557:

  559:             <p className="font-bold">
  560:               Assignments could not be

  580:

    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import Link from "next/link";
    4: import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";

    6:

   10: export default function ProgrammingPage() {
   11:   const router = useRouter();
   12: 
   13:   const {
   14:     user,
   15:     profile,
   16:     loading,
   17:     profileReady,
   18:     profileError,
   19:   } = useAuth();
   20: 
   21:   useEffect(() => {
   22:     if (!loading && !user) {
   23:       router.replace("/login");
   24:       return;
   25:     }
   26: 
   27:     if (
   28:       !loading &&
   29:       profileReady &&
   30:       profile?.role === "student" &&
   31:       (!profile.onboardingComplete ||
   32:         !profile.qualification ||
   33:         !profile.examBoard)
   34:     ) {
   35:       router.replace("/onboarding");
   36:     }
   37:   }, [
   38:     loading,
   39:     user,
   40:     profileReady,

   43:   ]);
   44:

   46:     loading ||
   47:     (user &&

   49:       !profileError)

   92:         <Link
   93:           href="/profile/curriculum"
   94:           className="mt-5 inline-flex rounded-xl bg-amber-600 px-5 py-3 font-black text-white"
   95:         >
   96:           Choose curriculum
   97:         </Link>
   98:       </section>
   99:     );
  100:   }

  103:     <div className="space-y-8">

FILE: app\teacher\programming-assignments\[assignmentId]\page.tsx
==============================================================================================================
    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import Link from "next/link";
    4: import { useParams } from "next/navigation";
    5: import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
    6: import { Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
    7: 
    8: import ProgrammingAssignmentResults from "@/components/teacher/programming/ProgrammingAssignmentResults";
    9: import { useAuth } from "@/contexts/AuthContext";
   10: import { getProgrammingAssignmentResults } from "@/services/programmingAssignmentService";
   11: import type { ProgrammingAssignmentResultsSummary } from "@/types/programmingAssignment";
   12: 
   13: function formatDate(value: Date | null): string {
   14:   if (!value) return "No due date";
   15: 
   16:   return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", {
   17:     day: "2-digit",
   18:     month: "long",
   19:     year: "numeric",
   20:   }).format(value);
   21: }
   22: 
   23: export default function ProgrammingAssignmentResultsPage() {
   24:   const params = useParams<{ assignmentId: string }>();
   25:   const { user } = useAuth();
   26: 
   27:   const [summary, setSummary] =
   28:     useState<ProgrammingAssignmentResultsSummary | null>(null);
   29: 
   30:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   31:   const [error, setError] = useState("");
   32: 
   33:   useEffect(() => {
   34:     if (!user?.uid || !params.assignmentId) {
   35:       return;
   36:     }
   37: 
   38:     let cancelled = false;
   39: 
   40:     async function load() {
   41:       try {
   42:         setLoading(true);
   43:         setError("");
   44: 
   45:         const loaded =
   46:           await getProgrammingAssignmentResults(
   47:             params.assignmentId,
   48:           );
   49: 
   50:         if (cancelled) return;
   51: 
   52:         if (!loaded) {
   53:           setError(
   54:             "This programming assignment could not be found.",
   55:           );
   56:           return;
   57:         }
   58: 
   59:         if (loaded.assignment.teacherId !== user?.uid) {
   60:           setError(
   61:             "You cannot view another teacher's programming assignment.",
   62:           );
   63:           return;
   64:         }
   65: 
   66:         setSummary(loaded);
   67:       } catch (caughtError) {
   68:         if (!cancelled) {
   69:           setError(
   70:             caughtError instanceof Error
   71:               ? caughtError.message
   72:               : "Programming results could not be loaded.",

  108:   }

  111: 
  112:   return (
  113:     <div className="space-y-7">
  114:       <section className="rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-br from-cyan-800 via-blue-800 to-indigo-800 p-8 text-white shadow-xl">

   10: 
   11: function formatDate(value: Date | null): string {
   12:   if (!value) return "No due date";
   13: 
   14:   return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", {
   15:     day: "2-digit",
   16:     month: "short",
   17:     year: "numeric",
   18:   }).format(value);
   19: }
   20: 
   21: export default function TeacherProgrammingAssignmentsPage() {
   22:   const { user } = useAuth();
   23: 
   24:   const [assignments, setAssignments] =
   25:     useState<ProgrammingAssignment[]>([]);
   26:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   27:   const [error, setError] = useState("");
   28:   const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
   29: 
   30:   const loadAssignments = useCallback(() => {
   31:     if (!user?.uid) {
   32:       return Promise.resolve().then(() => {
   33:         setAssignments([]);
   34:         setError("");
   35:         setLoading(false);
   36:       });
   37:     }
   38: 
   39:     const teacherId = user.uid;
   40: 
   41:     return Promise.resolve()
   42:       .then(() => {
   43:         setLoading(true);
   44:         setError("");
   45:         return getTeacherProgrammingAssignments(teacherId);
   46:       })
   47:       .then((loadedAssignments) => {
   48:         setAssignments(loadedAssignments);
   49:       })
   50:       .catch((caughtError) => {
   51:         setError(
   52:           caughtError instanceof Error
   53:             ? caughtError.message
   54:             : "Programming assignments could not be loaded.",
   55:         );
   56:       })
   57:       .finally(() => {
   58:         setLoading(false);
   59:       });
   60:   }, [user]);
   61: 
   62:   useEffect(() => {
   63:     void loadAssignments();
   64:   }, [loadAssignments]);
   65: 
   66:   const filtered = useMemo(() => {
   67:     const term = search.trim().toLowerCase();
   68: 
   69:     if (!term) return assignments;
   70: 
   71:     return assignments.filter(
   72:       (assignment) =>
   73:         assignment.title.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
   74:         assignment.className.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
   75:         assignment.topic.toLowerCase().includes(term),
   76:     );
   77:   }, [assignments, search]);
   78: 
   79:   const completed = assignments.reduce(
   80:     (total, assignment) => total + assignment.completedCount,
   81:     0,
   82:   );
   83: 
   84:   const students = assignments.reduce(
   85:     (total, assignment) => total + assignment.studentCount,
   86:     0,
   87:   );
   88: 
   89:   if (loading) {
   90:     return (
   91:       <main className="flex min-h-[65vh] items-center justify-center">
   92:         <Loader2 className="h-10 w-10 animate-spin text-cyan-700" />
   93:       </main>
   94:     );
   95:   }
   96: 
   97:   return (
   98:     <div className="space-y-7">
   99:       <section className="rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-br from-cyan-800 via-blue-800 to-indigo-800 p-8 text-white shadow-xl">
  100:         <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 lg:flex-row lg:items-end lg:justify-between">
  101:           <div>
  102:             <p className="text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-cyan-100">
  103:               Teacher programming
  104:             </p>
  105:             <h1 className="mt-2 text-4xl font-black">
  106:               Programming assignments
  107:             </h1>
  108:             <p className="mt-3 max-w-2xl text-cyan-50">
  109:               Assign exact coding tasks and review class completion,
  110:               attempts and test performance.
  111:             </p>
  112:           </div>
  113: 
  114:           <Link
  115:             href="/teacher/assignment-wizard"
  116:             className="inline-flex min-h-11 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-white px-5 py-3 font-black text-blue-800"
  117:           >
  118:             <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />
  119:             New assignment
  120:           </Link>
  121:         </div>
  122:       </section>

   18:     description: "Write code that passes visible and hidden tests.",
   19:   },
   20:   {
   21:     value: "debug",
   22:     title: "Debug",
   23:     description: "Repair broken programs and reason about the fault.",
   24:   },
   25:   {
   26:     value: "explore",
   27:     title: "Explore",
   28:     description: "Run your own Python in an isolated browser worker.",
   29:   },
   30: ];
   31: 
   32: export default function ProgrammingModeTabs({ value, onChange }: Props) {
   33:   return (
   34:     <section className="grid gap-3 lg:grid-cols-3">
   35:       {modes.map((mode) => {
   36:         const active = value === mode.value;
   37: 
   38:         return (
   39:           <button
   40:             key={mode.value}
   41:             type="button"
   42:             onClick={() => onChange(mode.value)}
   43:             className={`rounded-2xl border-2 p-5 text-left transition ${
   44:               active
   45:                 ? "border-blue-600 bg-blue-50"
   46:                 : "border-slate-200 bg-white hover:border-blue-300"
   47:             }`}
   48:           >
   49:             <p className="text-lg font-black text-slate-950">
   50:               {mode.title}
   51:             </p>
   52:             <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-600">
   53:               {mode.description}
   54:             </p>
   55:           </button>
   56:         );
   57:       })}
   58:     </section>
   59:   );
   60: }

FILE: components\programming\ProgrammingStats.tsx
==============================================================================================================
    1: type Props = {
    2:   attempts: number;
    3:   correct: number;
    4:   accuracy: number;
    5:   xp: number;
    6:   streak: number;
    7:   bestStreak: number;
    8: };
    9: 
   10: export default function ProgrammingStats({
   11:   attempts,
   12:   correct,
   13:   accuracy,
   14:   xp,
   15:   streak,
   16:   bestStreak,
   17: }: Props) {
   18:   const stats = [
   19:     ["Attempts", attempts.toString()],
   20:     ["Correct", correct.toString()],
   21:     ["Accuracy", `${accuracy}%`],
   22:     ["XP", xp.toString()],
   23:     ["Streak", `${streak}`],
   24:     ["Best streak", `${bestStreak}`],
   25:   ];
   26: 
   27:   return (
   28:     <section className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-6">
   29:       {stats.map(([label, value]) => (
   30:         <article
   31:           key={label}
   32:           className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-4"
   33:         >
   34:           <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-500">
   35:             {label}
   36:           </p>
   37:           <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">
   38:             {value}
   39:           </p>
   40:         </article>
   41:       ))}
   42:     </section>
   43:   );
   44: }

==============================================================================================================
FILE: components\programming\ProgrammingWorkspace.tsx

    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
    4: 
    5: import ProgrammingChallengePanel from "@/components/programming/ProgrammingChallengePanel";
    6: import ProgrammingConsole from "@/components/programming/ProgrammingConsole";
    7: import ProgrammingEditor from "@/components/programming/ProgrammingEditor";
    8: import ProgrammingModeTabs from "@/components/programming/ProgrammingModeTabs";
    9: import ProgrammingStats from "@/components/programming/ProgrammingStats";
   10: import SimulatorDifficulty from "@/components/Simulators/common/SimulatorDifficulty";
   11: import { useAuth } from "@/contexts/AuthContext";
   12: import { getProgrammingChallengeById } from "@/data/programming/challenges";
   13: import { useProgrammingProgress } from "@/hooks/useProgrammingProgress";
   14: import { evaluateProgrammingChallenge } from "@/lib/programming/evaluator";
   15: import { recordProgrammingAssignmentAttempt } from "@/services/programmingAssignmentService";
   16: import {
   17:   chooseNextProgrammingChallenge,
   18:   getProgrammingChallenges,
   19: } from "@/services/programmingChallengeService";
   20: import { runPython } from "@/services/pythonRunnerService";
   21: 
   22: import type {
   23:   ProgrammingChallenge,
   24:   ProgrammingDifficulty,
   25:   ProgrammingEvaluation,
   26:   ProgrammingExamBoard,
   27:   ProgrammingMode,
   28:   ProgrammingQualification,
   29:   PythonRunResult,
   30: } from "@/types/programming";
   31: 
   32: type ProgrammingWorkspaceProps = {
   33:   assignedChallengeId?: string | null;
   34:   assignmentId?: string | null;
   35:   assignmentTitle?: string;
   36: };
   37: 
   38: const exploreStarter = `name = input()
   39: print(f"Hello, {name}!")
   40: 
   41: for number in range(1, 4):
   42:     print(number)
   43: `;
   44: 
   45: const emptyRun: PythonRunResult = {
   46:   stdout: "",
   47:   stderr: "",
   48:   error: "",
   49:   timedOut: false,
   50:   durationMs: 0,
   51: };
   52: 
   53: function toQualification(
   54:   value: string | null | undefined,
   55: ): ProgrammingQualification {
   56:   return value === "A_LEVEL"
   57:     ? "A_LEVEL"
   58:     : "GCSE";
   59: }
   60: 
   61: function toExamBoard(
   62:   value: string | null | undefined,
   63: ): ProgrammingExamBoard | null {
   64:   if (
   65:     value === "AQA" ||
   66:     value === "OCR" ||
   67:     value === "EDEXCEL"
   68:   ) {
   69:     return value;
   70:   }
   71: 
   72:   return null;
   73: }
   74: 
   75: export default function ProgrammingWorkspace({
   76:   assignedChallengeId = null,
   77:   assignmentId = null,
   78:   assignmentTitle = "",
   79: }: ProgrammingWorkspaceProps = {}) {
   80:   const { user, profile } = useAuth();
   81: 
   82:   const qualification =
   83:     toQualification(
   84:       profile?.qualification,
   85:     );
   86: 
   87:   const examBoard =
   88:     toExamBoard(profile?.examBoard);
   89: 
   90:   const assignedChallenge =
   91:     useMemo(
   92:       () =>
   93:         assignedChallengeId
   94:           ? getProgrammingChallengeById(
   95:               assignedChallengeId,
   96:             )
   97:           : null,
   98:       [assignedChallengeId],
   99:     );
  100: 
  101:   const assignedMode =
  102:     Boolean(
  103:       assignmentId &&
  104:         assignedChallengeId,
  105:     );
  106: 
  107:   const [mode, setMode] =
  108:     useState<ProgrammingMode>(
  109:       assignedChallenge?.mode ??
  110:         "practice",
  111:     );
  112: 
  113:   const [difficulty, setDifficulty] =
  114:     useState<ProgrammingDifficulty>(
  115:       assignedChallenge?.difficulty ??
  116:         "foundation",
  117:     );
  118: 
  119:   const [
  120:     currentChallengeId,
  121:     setCurrentChallengeId,
  122:   ] = useState<string | null>(
  123:     assignedChallenge?.id ?? null,
  124:   );
  125: 
  126:   const progress =
  127:     useProgrammingProgress();
  128: 
  129:   useEffect(() => {
  130:     if (!assignedChallenge) return;
  131: 
  132:     let active = true;
  133: 
  134:     void Promise.resolve().then(() => {
  135:       if (!active) return;
  136: 
  137:       setMode(assignedChallenge.mode);
  138:       setDifficulty(
  139:         assignedChallenge.difficulty,
  140:       );
  141:       setCurrentChallengeId(
  142:         assignedChallenge.id,
  143:       );
  144:     });
  145: 
  146:     return () => {
  147:       active = false;
  148:     };
  149:   }, [assignedChallenge]);
  150: 
  151:   const availableChallenges =
  152:     useMemo(() => {
  153:       if (assignedMode) {
  154:         return assignedChallenge
  155:           ? [assignedChallenge]
  156:           : [];
  157:       }
  158: 
  159:       if (mode === "explore") {
  160:         return [];
  161:       }
  162: 
  163:       return getProgrammingChallenges({
  164:         qualification,
  165:         examBoard,
  166:         difficulty,
  167:         mode,
  168:       });
  169:     }, [
  170:       assignedChallenge,
  171:       assignedMode,
  172:       difficulty,
  173:       examBoard,
  174:       mode,
  175:       qualification,
  176:     ]);
  177: 
  178:   const challenge =
  179:     useMemo<ProgrammingChallenge | null>(
  180:       () => {
  181:         if (assignedMode) {
  182:           return assignedChallenge;
  183:         }
  184: 
  185:         if (mode === "explore") {
  186:           return null;
  187:         }
  188: 
  189:         const existing =
  190:           availableChallenges.find(
  191:             (item) =>
  192:               item.id ===
  193:               currentChallengeId,
  194:           ) ?? null;
  195: 
  196:         if (existing) return existing;
  197: 
  198:         return chooseNextProgrammingChallenge({
  199:           qualification,
  200:           examBoard,
  201:           difficulty,
  202:           mode,
  203:           completedChallengeIds:
  204:             progress.completedChallengeIds,
  205:           recentChallengeIds:
  206:             progress.history
  207:               .slice(0, 5)
  208:               .map(
  209:                 (item) =>
  210:                   item.challengeId,
  211:               ),
  212:           preferredWeakSkills:
  213:             progress.weakSkills,
  214:         });
  215:       },
  216:       [
  217:         assignedChallenge,
  218:         assignedMode,
  219:         availableChallenges,
  220:         currentChallengeId,
  221:         difficulty,
  222:         examBoard,
  223:         mode,
  224:         progress.completedChallengeIds,
  225:         progress.history,
  226:         progress.weakSkills,
  227:         qualification,
  228:       ],
  229:     );
  230: 
  231:   const [code, setCode] =
  232:     useState(exploreStarter);
  233: 
  234:   const [stdin, setStdin] =
  235:     useState("Ada");
  236: 
  237:   const [runResult, setRunResult] =
  238:     useState<PythonRunResult>(
  239:       emptyRun,
  240:     );
  241: 
  242:   const [
  243:     evaluation,
  244:     setEvaluation,
  245:   ] =
  246:     useState<ProgrammingEvaluation | null>(
  247:       null,
  248:     );
  249: 
  250:   const [running, setRunning] =
  251:     useState(false);
  252: 
  253:   const [checking, setChecking] =
  254:     useState(false);
  255: 
  256:   const [hintLevel, setHintLevel] =
  257:     useState<0 | 1 | 2>(0);
  258: 
  259:   const [
  260:     showExplanation,
  261:     setShowExplanation,
  262:   ] = useState(false);
  263: 
  264:   const [
  265:     statusMessage,
  266:     setStatusMessage,
  267:   ] = useState(
  268:     assignedMode
  269:       ? "Complete the assigned challenge and use Check solution when you are ready."
  270:       : "The first Python run may take longer while the browser loads the runtime.",
  271:   );
  272: 
  273:   useEffect(() => {
  274:     if (assignedMode) return;
  275: 
  276:     void Promise.resolve().then(() => {
  277:       setCurrentChallengeId(null);
  278:     });
  279:   }, [
  280:     assignedMode,
  281:     difficulty,
  282:     examBoard,
  283:     mode,
  284:     qualification,
  285:   ]);
  286: 
  287:   useEffect(() => {
  288:     void Promise.resolve().then(() => {
  289:       if (
  290:         mode === "explore" &&
  291:         !assignedMode
  292:       ) {
  293:         setCode(exploreStarter);
  294:         setStdin("Ada");
  295:       } else if (challenge) {
  296:         setCode(
  297:           challenge.starterCode,
  298:         );
  299: 
  300:         setStdin(
  301:           challenge.stdin ??
  302:             challenge.visibleTests[0]
  303:               ?.input ??
  304:             "",
  305:         );
  306: 
  307:         if (
  308:           challenge.id !==
  309:           currentChallengeId
  310:         ) {
  311:           setCurrentChallengeId(
  312:             challenge.id,
  313:           );
  314:         }
  315:       }
  316: 
  317:       setRunResult(emptyRun);
  318:       setEvaluation(null);
  319:       setHintLevel(0);
  320:       setShowExplanation(false);
  321:     });
  322:   }, [
  323:     assignedMode,
  324:     challenge,
  325:     currentChallengeId,
  326:     mode,
  327:   ]);
  328: 
  329:   async function handleRun() {
  330:     if (!code.trim()) return;
  331: 
  332:     setRunning(true);
  333:     setRunResult(emptyRun);
  334: 
  335:     setStatusMessage(
  336:       "Running Python in the browser...",
  337:     );
  338: 
  339:     try {
  340:       const result =
  341:         await runPython({
  342:           code,
  343:           stdin,
  344:           timeoutMs: 3000,
  345:         });
  346: 
  347:       setRunResult(result);
  348: 
  349:       if (result.timedOut) {
  350:         setStatusMessage(
  351:           "Execution stopped because it exceeded the time limit.",
  352:         );
  353:       } else if (
  354:         result.error ||
  355:         result.stderr
  356:       ) {
  357:         setStatusMessage(
  358:           "Python returned an error. Use the console to debug it.",
  359:         );
  360:       } else {
  361:         setStatusMessage(
  362:           "Run complete.",
  363:         );
  364:       }
  365:     } catch (error) {
  366:       setRunResult({
  367:         ...emptyRun,
  368:         error:
  369:           error instanceof Error
  370:             ? error.message
  371:             : "Python could not run.",
  372:       });
  373: 
  374:       setStatusMessage(
  375:         "The Python runtime could not start.",
  376:       );
  377:     } finally {
  378:       setRunning(false);
  379:     }
  380:   }
  381: 
  382:   async function handleCheck() {
  383:     if (
  384:       !challenge ||
  385:       !code.trim()
  386:     ) {
  387:       return;
  388:     }
  389: 
  390:     setChecking(true);
  391:     setEvaluation(null);
  392:     setShowExplanation(false);
  393: 
  394:     setStatusMessage(
  395:       "Checking visible and hidden tests...",
  396:     );
  397: 
  398:     try {
  399:       const result =
  400:         await evaluateProgrammingChallenge(
  401:           challenge,
  402:           code,
  403:         );
  404: 
  405:       setEvaluation(result);
  406: 
  407:       const awarded =
  408:         progress.recordAttempt(
  409:           challenge,
  410:           result.passed,
  411:         );
  412: 
  413:       if (
  414:         assignedMode &&
  415:         assignmentId &&
  416:         user?.uid
  417:       ) {
  418:         await recordProgrammingAssignmentAttempt(
  419:           {
  420:             assignmentId,
  421:             studentId: user.uid,
  422:             code,
  423:             passed: result.passed,
  424:             passedCount:
  425:               result.passedCount,
  426:             totalTests:
  427:               result.totalCount,
  428:             error:
  429:               result.results
  430:                 .filter(
  431:                   (item) =>
  432:                     !item.passed &&
  433:                     item.error,
  434:                 )
  435:                 .map(
  436:                   (item) =>
  437:                     item.error,
  438:                 )
  439:                 .filter(Boolean)
  440:                 .join("\n")
  441:                 .slice(0, 2000),
  442:           },
  443:         );
  444:       }
  445: 
  446:       if (result.passed) {
  447:         setShowExplanation(true);
  448: 
  449:         if (assignedMode) {
  450:           setStatusMessage(
  451:             awarded > 0
  452:               ? `Assignment completed. All tests passed and +${awarded} XP was awarded.`
  453:               : "Assignment completed. All tests passed.",
  454:           );
  455:         } else {
  456:           setStatusMessage(
  457:             awarded > 0
  458:               ? `All tests passed. +${awarded} XP awarded.`
  459:               : "All tests passed. This challenge was already completed for XP.",
  460:           );
  461:         }
  462:       } else {
  463:         setStatusMessage(
  464:           `${result.passedCount}/${result.totalCount} tests passed. Review the feedback and try again.`,
  465:         );
  466:       }
  467:     } catch (error) {
  468:       setStatusMessage(
  469:         error instanceof Error
  470:           ? error.message
  471:           : "The solution could not be checked.",
  472:       );
  473:     } finally {
  474:       setChecking(false);
  475:     }
  476:   }
  477: 
  478:   function resetCurrent() {
  479:     if (
  480:       mode === "explore" &&
  481:       !assignedMode
  482:     ) {
  483:       setCode(exploreStarter);
  484:       setStdin("Ada");
  485:     } else if (challenge) {
  486:       setCode(
  487:         challenge.starterCode,
  488:       );
  489: 
  490:       setStdin(
  491:         challenge.stdin ??
  492:           challenge.visibleTests[0]
  493:             ?.input ??
  494:           "",
  495:       );
  496:     }
  497: 
  498:     setRunResult(emptyRun);
  499:     setEvaluation(null);
  500:     setHintLevel(0);
  501:     setShowExplanation(false);
  502: 
  503:     setStatusMessage(
  504:       assignedMode
  505:         ? "Assigned challenge reset."
  506:         : "Challenge reset.",
  507:     );
  508:   }
  509: 
  510:   function nextChallenge() {
  511:     if (
  512:       assignedMode ||
  513:       mode === "explore" ||
  514:       availableChallenges.length === 0
  515:     ) {
  516:       return;
  517:     }
  518: 
  519:     const next =
  520:       chooseNextProgrammingChallenge({
  521:         qualification,
  522:         examBoard,
  523:         difficulty,
  524:         mode,
  525:         completedChallengeIds:
  526:           progress.completedChallengeIds,
  527:         recentChallengeIds: [
  528:           challenge?.id ?? "",
  529:           ...progress.history
  530:             .slice(0, 5)
  531:             .map(
  532:               (item) =>
  533:                 item.challengeId,
  534:             ),
  535:         ].filter(Boolean),
  536:         preferredWeakSkills:
  537:           progress.weakSkills,
  538:       });
  539: 
  540:     if (!next) {
  541:       setStatusMessage(
  542:         "No additional challenge is available for this combination yet.",
  543:       );
  544:       return;
  545:     }
  546: 
  547:     setCurrentChallengeId(
  548:       next.id,
  549:     );
  550: 
  551:     setStatusMessage(
  552:       "New curriculum-matched challenge loaded.",
  553:     );
  554:   }
  555: 
  556:   function requestHint() {
  557:     setHintLevel((current) => {
  558:       if (current === 0) return 1;
  559: 
  560:       if (
  561:         current === 1 &&
  562:         challenge?.secondHint
  563:       ) {
  564:         return 2;
  565:       }
  566: 
  567:       return current;
  568:     });
  569:   }
  570: 
  571:   return (
  572:     <div className="space-y-7">
  573:       {assignedMode && (
  574:         <section className="rounded-3xl border border-cyan-200 bg-cyan-50 p-6">
  575:           <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-cyan-700">
  576:             Teacher-assigned programming
  577:           </p>
  578: 
  579:           <h2 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black text-cyan-950">
  580:             {assignmentTitle ||
  581:               challenge?.title ||
  582:               "Programming assignment"}
  583:           </h2>
  584: 
  585:           <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-cyan-900">
  586:             This workspace is locked to the
  587:             exact challenge selected by your
  588:             teacher. Adaptive challenge
  589:             switching is disabled until you
  590:             leave this assignment.
  591:           </p>
  592:         </section>
  593:       )}
  594: 
  595:       <section className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-7 shadow-sm">
  596:         <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 xl:flex-row xl:items-end xl:justify-between">
  597:           <div>
  598:             <p className="text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-blue-600">
  599:               Live programming practice
  600:             </p>
  601: 
  602:             <h1 className="mt-2 text-4xl font-black text-slate-950">
  603:               Python Programming Workspace
  604:             </h1>
  605: 
  606:             <p className="mt-3 max-w-3xl leading-7 text-slate-600">
  607:               Write, run, debug and test Python
  608:               directly in your browser.
  609:             </p>
  610:           </div>
  611: 
  612:           <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-950 px-5 py-4 text-white">
  613:             <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400">
  614:               Current curriculum
  615:             </p>
  616: 
  617:             <p className="mt-1 font-black">
  618:               {examBoard ?? "General"}{" "}
  619:               {qualification ===
  620:               "A_LEVEL"
  621:                 ? "A-Level"
  622:                 : "GCSE"}
  623:             </p>
  624:           </div>
  625:         </div>
  626:       </section>
  627: 
  628:       {!assignedMode && (
  629:         <ProgrammingModeTabs
  630:           value={mode}
  631:           onChange={setMode}
  632:         />
  633:       )}
  634: 
  635:       {!assignedMode &&
  636:         mode !== "explore" && (
  637:           <SimulatorDifficulty
  638:             value={difficulty}
  639:             onChange={
  640:               setDifficulty
  641:             }
  642:           />
  643:         )}
  644: 
  645:       <ProgrammingStats
  646:         attempts={progress.attempts}
  647:         correct={progress.correct}
  648:         accuracy={progress.accuracy}
  649:         xp={progress.xp}
  650:         streak={progress.streak}
  651:         bestStreak={
  652:           progress.bestStreak
  653:         }
  654:       />
  655: 
  656:       {!assignedMode &&
  657:         progress.weakSkills.length >
  658:           0 && (
  659:           <section className="rounded-2xl border border-violet-200 bg-violet-50 px-5 py-4">
  660:             <p className="font-black text-violet-950">
  661:               Adaptive focus
  662:             </p>
  663: 
  664:             <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-violet-800">
  665:               CS Master is
  666:               prioritising:{" "}
  667:               {progress.weakSkills.join(
  668:                 ", ",
  669:               )}
  670:               .
  671:             </p>
  672:           </section>
  673:         )}
  674: 
  675:       <section className="rounded-2xl border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 px-5 py-4">
  676:         <p className="font-bold text-blue-950">
  677:           {statusMessage}
  678:         </p>
  679:       </section>
  680: 
  681:       {mode !== "explore" && (

  687:             showExplanation

  689:         />
  690:       )}
  691: 
  692:       {!assignedMode &&
  693:         mode === "explore" && (

  696:               Explore mode
  697:             </p>
  698: 
  699:             <h2 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black text-emerald-950">
  700:               Your own Python
  701:             </h2>
  702: 
  703:             <p className="mt-3 leading-7 text-emerald-900">
  704:               Change the program and standard
  705:               input freely.
  706:             </p>
  707:           </section>
  708:         )}
  709: 
  710:       <div className="grid gap-6 xl:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1.35fr)_minmax(340px,0.65fr)]">

  714:           disabled={
  715:             running || checking
  716:           }

   92:     description,
   93:     resourceType: type,
   94:     resourceId: type,
   95:   };
   96: }
   97: 
   98: export default function AssignmentResourceStep({
   99:   selectedResource,
  100:   onSelect,
  101:   onNext,
  102: }: AssignmentResourceStepProps) {
  103:   const { user, loading: authLoading } = useAuth();
  104: 
  105:   const [selectedType, setSelectedType] =
  106:     useState<AssignmentResourceType | null>(
  107:       selectedResource?.resourceType || null,
  108:     );
  109: 
  110:   const [savedQuizzes, setSavedQuizzes] =
  111:     useState<SavedQuizOption[]>([]);
  112:   const [loadingQuizzes, setLoadingQuizzes] = useState(true);
  113:   const [quizLoadError, setQuizLoadError] = useState("");
  114: 
  115:   useEffect(() => {
  116:     void Promise.resolve().then(() => {
  117:       setSelectedType(selectedResource?.resourceType || null);
  118:     });
  119:   }, [selectedResource]);
  120: 
  121:   useEffect(() => {
  122:     if (authLoading) return;
  123: 
  124:     if (!user) {
  125:       void Promise.resolve().then(() => {
  126:         setSavedQuizzes([]);
  127:         setLoadingQuizzes(false);
  128:       });
  129:       return;
  130:     }
  131: 
  132:     const quizzesQuery = query(
  133:       collection(db, "generatedQuizzes"),
  134:       where("teacherId", "==", user.uid),
  135:     );
  136: 
  137:     const unsubscribe = onSnapshot(
  138:       quizzesQuery,
  139:       (snapshot) => {
  140:         const loadedQuizzes: SavedQuizOption[] = snapshot.docs.map(
  141:           (quizDocument) => {
  142:             const data = quizDocument.data();
  143: 
  144:             return {
  145:               id: quizDocument.id,
  146:               title: data.title || "Untitled Quiz",
  147:               description:
  148:                 data.description || "Complete the assigned quiz.",
  149:               qualification: data.qualification || "GCSE",
  150:               examBoard: data.examBoard || "AQA",
  151:               difficulty: data.difficulty || "standard",
  152:               questionCount:
  153:                 typeof data.questionCount === "number"
  154:                   ? data.questionCount
  155:                   : Array.isArray(data.questions)
  156:                     ? data.questions.length
  157:                     : 0,
  158:             };
  159:           },
  160:         );
  161: 
  162:         loadedQuizzes.sort((a, b) => a.title.localeCompare(b.title));
  163:         setSavedQuizzes(loadedQuizzes);
  164:         setQuizLoadError("");
  165:         setLoadingQuizzes(false);
  166:       },
  167:       (error) => {
  168:         console.error("Failed to load saved quizzes:", error);
  169:         setSavedQuizzes([]);
  170:         setQuizLoadError("Could not load your saved quizzes.");
  171:         setLoadingQuizzes(false);
  172:       },
  173:     );
  174: 
  175:     return unsubscribe;
  176:   }, [authLoading, user]);
  177: 
  178:   function chooseResourceType(option: {
  179:     type: AssignmentResourceType;
  180:     title: string;
  181:     description: string;
  182:   }) {
  183:     setSelectedType(option.type);
  184: 
  185:     if (
  186:       option.type === "lesson" ||
  187:       option.type === "quiz" ||
  188:       option.type === "ai-quiz" ||
  189:       option.type === "exam-paper" ||
  190:       option.type === "programming-challenge" ||
  191:       option.type === "teaching-resource"
  192:     ) {
  193:       if (selectedResource?.resourceType !== option.type) {
  194:         onSelect(
  195:           createPlaceholderResource(
  196:             option.type,
  197:             option.type === "lesson"
  198:               ? "Choose a Lesson"
  199:               : option.type === "quiz"
  200:                 ? "Choose an Existing Quiz"
  201:                 : option.type === "exam-paper"
  202:                 ? "Choose an Exam Paper"
  203:                 : option.type === "programming-challenge"
  204:                   ? "Choose a Programming Challenge"
  205:                   : option.type === "teaching-resource"
  206:                     ? "Choose a Teaching Resource"
  207:                     : "Choose an AI Quiz",
  208:             option.type === "lesson"
  209:               ? "Select an exact curriculum lesson from the library below."
  210:               : option.type === "quiz"
  211:                 ? "Select an exact built-in quiz from the library below."
  212:                 : option.type === "exam-paper"
  213:                 ? "Select an exact saved paper from your Question Bank below."
  214:                 : option.type === "programming-challenge"
  215:                   ? "Select an exact challenge from the library below."
  216:                   : option.type === "teaching-resource"
  217:                     ? "Select a published teacher resource from the library below."
  218:                     : "Select a saved quiz from the list below.",
  219:           ),
  220:         );
  221:       }
  222: 
  223:       return;
  224:     }
  225: 
  226:     onSelect(
  227:       createPlaceholderResource(
  228:         option.type,
  229:         option.title,
  230:         option.description,
  231:       ),
  232:     );
  233:   }
  234: 
  235:   function chooseSavedQuiz(quiz: SavedQuizOption) {
  236:     setSelectedType("ai-quiz");
  237:     onSelect({
  238:       id: quiz.id,
  239:       title: quiz.title,
  240:       description: quiz.description,
  241:       resourceType: "ai-quiz",
  242:       resourceId: quiz.id,
  243:       questionCount: quiz.questionCount,
  244:     });
  245:   }
  246: 
  247:   const validResourceSelected =
  248:     selectedResource !== null &&
  249:     !(selectedResource.resourceType === "lesson" && selectedResource.resourceId === "lesson") &&
  250:     !(selectedResource.resourceType === "quiz" && selectedResource.resourceId === "quiz") &&
  251:     !(selectedResource.resourceType === "ai-quiz" && selectedResource.resourceId === "ai-quiz") &&
  252:     !(selectedResource.resourceType === "exam-paper" && selectedResource.resourceId === "exam-paper") &&
  253:     !(
  254:       selectedResource.resourceType === "programming-challenge" &&
  255:       selectedResource.resourceId === "programming-challenge"
  256:     ) &&
  257:     !(selectedResource.resourceType === "teaching-resource" && selectedResource.resourceId === "teaching-resource");
  258: 
  259:   return (
  260:     <Card>
  261:       <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-teal-600">
  262:         Step 1 of 4
  263:       </p>
  264: 
  265:       <h2 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">
  266:         Choose Assignment Type
  267:       </h2>
  268: 
  269:       <p className="mt-2 text-slate-600">
  270:         Select the type of resource you want to assign.
  271:       </p>
  272: 
  273:       <div className="mt-6 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
  274:         {resourceOptions.map((option) => {
  275:           const selected = selectedType === option.type;
  276: 
  277:           return (
  278:             <button
  279:               key={option.type}
  280:               type="button"
  281:               onClick={() => chooseResourceType(option)}
  282:               className={`rounded-2xl border p-5 text-left transition ${
  283:                 selected
  284:                   ? "border-teal-500 bg-teal-50 ring-2 ring-teal-100"
  285:                   : "border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 hover:border-teal-300 hover:bg-teal-50/50"
  286:               }`}
  287:             >
  288:               <div className="text-4xl">{option.icon}</div>
  289:               <h3 className="mt-4 text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">
  290:                 {option.title}
  291:               </h3>
  292:               <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-600">
  293:                 {option.description}
  294:               </p>
  295:             </button>
  296:           );
  297:         })}
  298:       </div>
  299: 
  300:       {selectedType === "lesson" && (
  301:         <LessonAssignmentSelector
  302:           selectedResource={selectedResource}
  303:           onSelect={(resource) => {
  304:             setSelectedType("lesson");
  305:             onSelect(resource);
  306:           }}
  307:         />
  308:       )}
  309: 
  310:       {selectedType === "quiz" && (
  311:         <ExistingQuizSelector
  312:           selectedResource={selectedResource}
  313:           onSelect={(resource) => {
  314:             setSelectedType("quiz");
  315:             onSelect(resource);
  316:           }}
  317:         />
  318:       )}
  319:

  324:           </p>
  325:           <h3 className="mt-2 text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">
  326:             Choose a Saved Quiz
  327:           </h3>
  328: 
  329:           {loadingQuizzes ? (
  330:             <div className="mt-6 space-y-3">
  331:               <div className="h-28 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-white" />
  332:               <div className="h-28 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-white" />
  333:             </div>

   18:   value: string,
   19: ): string {
   20:   return value
   21:     .replace(/[-_]/g, " ")
   22:     .replace(/\b\w/g, (character) =>
   23:       character.toUpperCase(),
   24:     );

  199:             </p>

  210:         >
  211:           â† Back
  212:         </button>

  215:           type="button"
  216:           onClick={onSubmit}
  217:           disabled={
  218:             submitting ||

  220:             data.selectedClassIds.length === 0 ||
  221:             !data.dueDate ||
  222:             !data.instructions.trim()

  224:           className="rounded-xl bg-teal-600 px-6 py-3 font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-teal-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
  225:         >
  226:           {submitting
  227:             ? "Creating Assignments..."
  228:             : `Assign to ${data.selectedClassIds.length} ${
  229:                 data.selectedClassIds.length === 1
  230:                   ? "Class"
  231:                   : "Classes"
  232:               }`}
  233:         </button>
  234:       </div>
  235:     </Card>
  236:   );

  239: function ReviewSection({
  240:   label,

    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
    4: import Link from "next/link";
    5: 
    6: import { useAuth } from "@/contexts/AuthContext";
    7: import {
    8:   getTeacherExamQuestionSets,
    9:   type SavedExamQuestionSet,
   10: } from "@/services/examQuestionService";
   11: import type { AssignmentWizardResource } from "@/types/assignmentWizard";
   12: 
   13: type Props = {
   14:   selectedResource: AssignmentWizardResource | null;
   15:   onSelect: (resource: AssignmentWizardResource) => void;
   16: };
   17: 
   18: function cleanLabel(value: string): string {
   19:   return value
   20:     .replace(/[-_]/g, " ")
   21:     .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
   22:     .trim();
   23: }
   24: 
   25: function searchText(questionSet: SavedExamQuestionSet): string {
   26:   const content = questionSet.content;
   27: 
   28:   return [
   29:     questionSet.id,
   30:     questionSet.title,
   31:     content.title,
   32:     content.description,
   33:     content.topic,
   34:     content.qualification,
   35:     content.examBoard,
   36:   ]
   37:     .filter(Boolean)
   38:     .join(" ")
   39:     .toLowerCase();
   40: }
   41: 
   42: export default function ExamPaperAssignmentSelector({
   43:   selectedResource,
   44:   onSelect,
   45: }: Props) {
   46:   const { user } = useAuth();
   47: 
   48:   const [questionSets, setQuestionSets] =
   49:     useState<SavedExamQuestionSet[]>([]);
   50: 
   51:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   52:   const [error, setError] = useState("");
   53:   const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
   54: 
   55:   useEffect(() => {
   56:     let cancelled = false;
   57: 
   58:     async function loadQuestionSets() {
   59:       if (!user?.uid) {
   60:         if (!cancelled) {
   61:           setQuestionSets([]);
   62:           setLoading(false);
   63:         }
   64: 
   65:         return;
   66:       }
   67: 
   68:       try {
   69:         setLoading(true);
   70:         setError("");
   71: 
   72:         const loaded =
   73:           await getTeacherExamQuestionSets(
   74:             user.uid,
   75:           );
   76: 
   77:         if (cancelled) return;
   78: 
   79:         setQuestionSets(
   80:           [...loaded].sort((a, b) =>
   81:             a.title.localeCompare(
   82:               b.title,
   83:             ),
   84:           ),
   85:         );
   86:       } catch (caughtError) {
   87:         console.error(
   88:           "Unable to load saved exam papers:",
   89:           caughtError,
   90:         );
   91: 
   92:         if (!cancelled) {
   93:           setQuestionSets([]);
   94:           setError(
   95:             caughtError instanceof Error
   96:               ? caughtError.message
   97:               : "The Question Bank could not be loaded.",
   98:           );
   99:         }
  100:       } finally {
  101:         if (!cancelled) {
  102:           setLoading(false);
  103:         }
  104:       }
  105:     }
  106: 
  107:     void loadQuestionSets();
  108: 
  109:     return () => {
  110:       cancelled = true;
  111:     };
  112:   }, [user?.uid]);
  113: 
  114:   const filtered = useMemo(() => {
  115:     const term =
  116:       search.trim().toLowerCase();
  117: 
  118:     if (!term) {
  119:       return questionSets;
  120:     }
  121: 
  122:     return questionSets.filter(
  123:       (questionSet) =>
  124:         searchText(
  125:           questionSet,
  126:         ).includes(term),
  127:     );
  128:   }, [questionSets, search]);
  129: 
  130:   return (
  131:     <section className="mt-8 rounded-3xl border border-indigo-200 bg-indigo-50/50 p-5 sm:p-6">
  132:       <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-indigo-700">
  133:         Question Bank
  134:       </p>
  135: 
  136:       <h3 className="mt-2 text-xl font-black text-slate-950">
  137:         Choose the exact exam paper
  138:       </h3>
  139: 
  140:       <p className="mt-2 max-w-3xl text-sm leading-6 text-slate-600">
  141:         Select a saved exam-style question set.
  142:         CS Master will preserve the exact
  143:         questions, marks, mark scheme and model
  144:         answers in the class assignment.
  145:       </p>
  146: 
  147:       <div className="mt-5 flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between">
  148:         <label className="block w-full max-w-xl">
  149:           <span className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
  150:             Search Question Bank
  151:           </span>
  152: 
  153:           <input
  154:             type="search"
  155:             value={search}
  156:             onChange={(event) =>
  157:               setSearch(
  158:                 event.target.value,
  159:               )
  160:             }
  161:             placeholder="Paper, topic, exam board..."
  162:             className="mt-2 min-h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-4 text-sm outline-none focus:border-indigo-500 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-indigo-100"
  163:           />
  164:         </label>
  165: 
  166:         <Link
  167:           href="/teacher/question-bank"
  168:           className="inline-flex min-h-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-indigo-200 bg-white px-4 py-2 text-sm font-bold text-indigo-700 transition hover:bg-indigo-50"
  169:         >
  170:           Open Question Bank
  171:         </Link>
  172:       </div>
  173: 
  174:       {loading ? (
  175:         <div className="mt-6 space-y-3">
  176:           <div className="h-32 animate-pulse rounded-2xl bg-white" />
  177:           <div className="h-32 animate-pulse rounded-2xl bg-white" />
  178:         </div>
  179:       ) : error ? (
  180:         <div className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-5 text-sm font-semibold text-red-700">
  181:           {error}
  182:         </div>
  183:       ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
  184:         <div className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 text-center">
  185:           <p className="font-bold text-slate-900">
  186:             No saved exam papers found
  187:           </p>
  188: 
  189:           <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
  190:             Save an exam-style paper to the Question
  191:             Bank first, then return here.
  192:           </p>
  193: 
  194:           <a
  195:             href="/teacher/exam-question-generator"
  196:             className="mt-4 inline-flex rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white"
  197:           >
  198:             Generate Exam Paper
  199:           </a>
  200:         </div>
  201:       ) : (
  202:         <div className="mt-6 grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-2">
  203:           {filtered.map(
  204:             (questionSet) => {
  205:               const content =
  206:                 questionSet.content;
  207: 
  208:               const selected =
  209:                 selectedResource?.resourceType ===
  210:                   "exam-paper" &&
  211:                 selectedResource.resourceId ===
  212:                   questionSet.id;
  213: 
  214:               return (
  215:                 <button
  216:                   key={questionSet.id}
  217:                   type="button"
  218:                   onClick={() =>
  219:                     onSelect({
  220:                       id: questionSet.id,
  221:                       title:
  222:                         questionSet.title ||
  223:                         content.title,
  224:                       description:
  225:                         content.description ||
  226:                         `Written assessment on ${content.topic}.`,
  227:                       resourceType:
  228:                         "exam-paper",
  229:                       resourceId:
  230:                         questionSet.id,
  231:                       examTopic:
  232:                         content.topic,
  233:                       examQualification:
  234:                         content.qualification,
  235:                       examBoard:
  236:                         content.examBoard,
  237:                       questionCount:
  238:                         content.questionCount,
  239:                       totalMarks:
  240:                         content.totalMarks,
  241:                     })
  242:                   }
  243:                   className={`rounded-2xl border p-5 text-left transition ${
  244:                     selected
  245:                       ? "border-indigo-600 bg-white ring-2 ring-indigo-100"
  246:                       : "border-indigo-100 bg-white hover:border-indigo-300"
  247:                   }`}
  248:                 >
  249:                   <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
  250:                     <div>
  251:                       <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wide text-indigo-700">
  252:                         {content.examBoard} Ã‚Â·{" "}
  253:                         {cleanLabel(
  254:                           content.qualification,
  255:                         )}
  256:                       </p>
  257: 
  258:                       <h4 className="mt-2 text-lg font-black text-slate-950">
  259:                         {questionSet.title ||
  260:                           content.title}
  261:                       </h4>
  262:

FILE: components\teacher\exam-assignments\AssignExamModal.tsx
==============================================================================================================
    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
    4: import toast from "react-hot-toast";
    5: 
    6: import { useAuth } from "@/contexts/AuthContext";
    7: import { getTeacherClasses, type TeacherClass } from "@/services/classService";
    8: import { createExamAssignment } from "@/services/examAssignmentService";
    9: import type { SavedExamQuestionSet } from "@/services/examQuestionService";
   10: 
   11: type Props = {
   12:   questionSet: SavedExamQuestionSet;
   13:   onClose: () => void;
   14:   onAssigned?: (assignmentId: string) => void;
   15: };
   16: 
   17: export default function AssignExamModal({
   18:   questionSet,
   19:   onClose,
   20:   onAssigned,
   21: }: Props) {
   22:   const { user } = useAuth();
   23: 
   24:   const [classes, setClasses] = useState<TeacherClass[]>([]);
   25:   const [classId, setClassId] = useState("");
   26:   const [title, setTitle] = useState(questionSet.title);
   27:   const [instructions, setInstructions] = useState(
   28:     "Answer every question. Show working where required.",
   29:   );
   30:   const [dueDate, setDueDate] = useState("");
   31:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   32:   const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
   33: 
   34:   useEffect(() => {
   35:     async function load() {
   36:       if (!user?.uid) {
   37:         return;
   38:       }
   39: 
   40:       try {
   41:         const loaded = await getTeacherClasses(user.uid);
   42: 
   43:         setClasses(loaded.filter((item) => item.status === "active"));
   44:       } catch (error) {
   45:         console.error(error);
   46:         toast.error("Classes could not be loaded.");
   47:       } finally {
   48:         setLoading(false);
   49:       }
   50:     }
   51: 
   52:     void load();
   53:   }, [user?.uid]);
   54: 
   55:   async function assign() {
   56:     if (!user?.uid) {
   57:       toast.error("You must be logged in.");
   58:       return;
   59:     }
   60: 
   61:     const selectedClass = classes.find((item) => item.id === classId);
   62: 
   63:     if (!selectedClass) {
   64:       toast.error("Select a class.");
   65:       return;
   66:     }
   67: 
   68:     if (!dueDate) {
   69:       toast.error("Select a due date.");
   70:       return;
   71:     }
   72: 
   73:     setSaving(true);
   74: 
   75:     try {
   76:       const assignmentId = await createExamAssignment({
   77:         teacherId: user.uid,
   78:         teacherName: user.displayName || "Teacher",
   79:         classId: selectedClass.id,
   80:         className: selectedClass.name,
   81:         studentIds: selectedClass.studentIds,
   82:         questionSetId: questionSet.id,
   83:         questionSetTitle: questionSet.title,
   84:         questionSetSnapshot: questionSet.content,
   85:         title,
   86:         instructions,
   87:         dueDate: new Date(`${dueDate}T23:59:59`),
   88:       });
   89: 
   90:       toast.success("Exam assignment created.");
   91: 
   92:       onAssigned?.(assignmentId);
   93:     } catch (error) {
   94:       console.error(error);
   95:       toast.error(
   96:         error instanceof Error
   97:           ? error.message
   98:           : "Assignment could not be created.",
   99:       );
  100:     } finally {
  101:       setSaving(false);
  102:     }
  103:   }
  104: 
  105:   return (
  106:     <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-950/60 p-4">
  107:       <div className="w-full max-w-2xl rounded-3xl bg-white p-6 shadow-2xl">
  108:         <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
  109:           <div>
  110:             <p className="text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-indigo-600">
  111:               Assign assessment
  112:             </p>
  113: 
  114:             <h2 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">
  115:               {questionSet.title}
  116:             </h2>
  117:           </div>
  118: 
  119:           <button
  120:             type="button"
  121:             onClick={onClose}
  122:             className="rounded-lg border px-3 py-2 font-bold"
  123:           >
  124:             Close
  125:           </button>
  126:         </div>
  127: 
  128:         <div className="mt-6 space-y-5">
  129:           <label className="block">
  130:             <span className="text-sm font-bold">Class</span>
  131: 
  132:             <select
  133:               value={classId}
  134:               onChange={(event) => setClassId(event.target.value)}
  135:               disabled={loading}
  136:               className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border px-4 py-3"
  137:             >
  138:               <option value="">Select a class</option>
  139: 
  140:               {classes.map((item) => (
  141:                 <option key={item.id} value={item.id}>
  142:                   {item.name} ({item.studentIds.length} students)
  143:                 </option>
  144:               ))}
  145:             </select>
  146:           </label>
  147: 
  148:           <label className="block">
  149:             <span className="text-sm font-bold">Assignment title</span>
  150: 
  151:             <input
  152:               value={title}
  153:               onChange={(event) => setTitle(event.target.value)}
  154:               className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border px-4 py-3"
  155:             />
  156:           </label>
  157: 
  158:           <label className="block">
  159:             <span className="text-sm font-bold">Instructions</span>
  160: 
  161:             <textarea
  162:               rows={4}
  163:               value={instructions}
  164:               onChange={(event) => setInstructions(event.target.value)}
  165:               className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border px-4 py-3"
  166:             />
  167:           </label>
  168: 
  169:           <label className="block">
  170:             <span className="text-sm font-bold">Due date</span>
  171: 
  172:             <input
  173:               type="date"
  174:               value={dueDate}
  175:               onChange={(event) => setDueDate(event.target.value)}
  176:               className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border px-4 py-3"
  177:             />
  178:           </label>
  179:         </div>
  180: 
  181:         <div className="mt-7 flex justify-end gap-3">
  182:           <button
  183:             type="button"
  184:             onClick={onClose}
  185:             className="rounded-xl border px-5 py-3 font-bold"
  186:           >
  187:             Cancel
  188:           </button>
  189: 
  190:           <button
  191:             type="button"
  192:             onClick={() => {
  193:               void assign();
  194:             }}
  195:             disabled={saving}
  196:             className="rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 px-5 py-3 font-bold text-white disabled:bg-slate-300"
  197:           >
  198:             {saving ? "Assigning..." : "Assign to Class"}
  199:           </button>
  200:         </div>
  201:       </div>
  202:     </div>
  203:   );
  204: }

   29: 
   30: function percentage(
   31:   value: number | null,
   32: ): string {
   33:   return value === null
   34:     ? "Ã¢â‚¬â€"
   35:     : `${value}%`;
   36: }
   37: 
   38: function priorityClass(
   39:   value:
   40:     | "high"
   41:     | "medium"
   42:     | "monitor"
   43:     | "none",
   44: ): string {
   45:   if (value === "high") {
   46:     return "bg-red-100 text-red-700";
   47:   }
   48: 
   49:   if (value === "medium") {
   50:     return "bg-amber-100 text-amber-700";
   51:   }
   52: 
   53:   if (value === "monitor") {
   54:     return "bg-blue-100 text-blue-700";
   55:   }
   56: 
   57:   return "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700";
   58: }
   59: 
   60: function aoDescription(
   61:   item: ExamAssessmentObjectiveIntelligence,
   62: ): string {
   63:   if (
   64:     item.assessmentObjective ===
   65:     "AO1"
   66:   ) {
   67:     return "Knowledge and understanding";
   68:   }
   69: 
   70:   if (
   71:     item.assessmentObjective ===
   72:     "AO2"
   73:   ) {
   74:     return "Application of knowledge and understanding";
   75:   }
   76: 
   77:   return "Analysis, evaluation and problem solving";
   78: }
   79: 
   80: export default function ExamAssignmentIntelligencePanel({
   81:   assignment,
   82:   submissions,
   83: }: {
   84:   assignment: ExamAssignment;
   85:   submissions: ExamSubmission[];
   86: }) {

   88:     buildExamClassIntelligence(
   89:       assignment,

   95:

   97:     intelligence.studentPriorities.filter(
   98:       (student) =>
   99:         student.priority !== "none",
  100:     );
  101: 
  102:   const weakestTopic =
  103:     intelligence.weakestTopic?.topic ||
  104:     assignment.questionSetSnapshot.topic ||
  105:     assignment.questionSetTitle;
  106: 
  107:   const classKnowledgeMapHref =
  108:     `/teacher/knowledge-map?source=exam` +
  109:     `&assignmentId=${encodeURIComponent(assignment.id)}` +
  110:     `&classId=${encodeURIComponent(assignment.classId)}` +
  111:     `&topic=${encodeURIComponent(weakestTopic)}`;
  112: 
  113:   const followUpHref =
  114:     `/teacher/assignment-wizard?source=exam` +
  115:     `&classId=${encodeURIComponent(assignment.classId)}` +
  116:     `&topic=${encodeURIComponent(weakestTopic)}`;
  117: 
  118:   return (
  119:     <div className="space-y-6">
  120:       <Card className="overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 p-0">
  121:         <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-slate-950 via-indigo-950 to-violet-900 p-6 text-white">

  125:               Exam intelligence

  134:             Grade provenance, question facility, marks lost, topic and AO
  135:             performance, boundary proximity and sample-size safeguards.
  136:           </p>
  137:         </div>
  138: 
  139:         <div className="grid gap-4 p-6 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-7">
  140:           <Metric label="Submission" value={`${intelligence.submissionPercentage}%`} />
  141:           <Metric label="Marking" value={`${intelligence.markingPercentage}%`} />
  142:           <Metric label="Class average" value={percentage(grade.classAveragePercentage)} />
  143:           <Metric label="Median" value={percentage(intelligence.medianPercentage)} />
  144:           <Metric label="Highest" value={percentage(intelligence.highestPercentage)} />
  145:           <Metric label="Lowest" value={percentage(intelligence.lowestPercentage)} />
  146:           <Metric label="Integrity events" value={String(intelligence.integrity.totalIncidents)} />
  147:         </div>
  148:       </Card>

  682:                             className="flex items-start gap-2"
  683:                           >
  684:                             <AlertTriangle className="mt-0.5 h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-amber-600" />
  685:                             {reasonItem}
  686:                           </li>
  687:                         ))}
  688:                       </ul>
  689:                     )}
  690:                   </div>
  691:                 );
  692:               })}
  693:             </div>
  694:           )}
  695:         </Card>
  696:       </div>
  697:     </div>
  698:   );
  699: }
  700: 
  701: function Metric({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  702:   return (
  703:     <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 p-4">
  704:       <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-400">{label}</p>
  705:       <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">{value}</p>
  706:     </div>
  707:   );
  708: }
  709: 
  710: function SmallMetric({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  711:   return (
  712:     <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-4 shadow-sm">
  713:       <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-400">{label}</p>
  714:       <p className="mt-2 text-xl font-black text-slate-950">{value}</p>
  715:     </div>
  716:   );
  717: }
  718: 
  719: function MiniMetric({ label, value }: { label: string; value: number }) {
  720:   return (
  721:     <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 p-4">
  722:       <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-400">{label}</p>
  723:       <p className="mt-1 text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">{value}</p>
  724:     </div>
  725:   );

   37:   const [source, setSource] =
   38:     useState<AnalyticsBoundarySource>(

   54:     assessmentTitle,

   64:         "",
   65:     );
   66:

   68:     useState(false);

   71:     () =>
   72:       gradeIntelligence.boundaries
   73:         .filter(
   74:           (boundary) =>
   75:             boundary.grade !== "U",
   76:         )

   78:           grade: boundary.grade,
   79:           minimumMark:
   80:             String(
   81:               boundary.minimumMark,
   82:             ),

   85:   );
