   89: 
   90:   function updateRow(
   91:     grade: GradeLabel,
   92:     value: string,
   93:   ) {
   94:     setRows((current) =>
   95:       current.map((row) =>
   96:         row.grade === grade
   97:           ? {
   98:               ...row,
   99:               minimumMark: value,
  100:             }
  101:           : row,
  102:       ),
  103:     );
  104:   }
  105:

  111:         assignmentId:
  112:           assignment.id,
  113:         teacherId:
  114:           assignment.teacherId,

  131:               Number(row.minimumMark),

  137:       );

  139:       window.location.reload();

  142:         error instanceof Error
  143:           ? error.message
  144:           : "Grade boundaries could not be saved.",
  145:       );
  146:     } finally {
  147:       setSaving(false);
  148:     }
  149:   }
  150:

  152:     try {
  153:       setSaving(true);
  154: 
  155:       await clearExamBoundaryConfiguration({
  156:         assignmentId:
  157:           assignment.id,
  158:         teacherId:
  159:           assignment.teacherId,

  172:       );
  173:     } finally {
  174:       setSaving(false);
  175:     }
  176:   }
  177: 
  178:   return (

  180:       <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-950 to-violet-800 p-6 text-white">

  194:           Use verified official boundaries when this is a known exam paper,

    1: "use client";

    7: } from "lucide-react";

   13:   ExamAssignment,

   15:   ExamVisibilityAction,
   16: } from "@/types/examAssignment";
   17: 
   18: export default function ExamIntegrityPolicyCard({
   19:   assignment,
   20:   teacherId,
   21:   onSaved,
   22: }: {
   23:   assignment: ExamAssignment;
   24:   teacherId: string;
   25:   onSaved: (
   26:     policy: ExamIntegrityPolicy,
   27:   ) => void;
   28: }) {
   29:   const [policy, setPolicy] =
   30:     useState<ExamIntegrityPolicy>(
   31:       assignment.integrityPolicy,
   32:     );
   33: 
   34:   const [saving, setSaving] =
   35:     useState(false);
   36: 
   37:   async function save() {
   38:     try {
   39:       setSaving(true);
   40: 
   41:       const updated =
   42:         await updateExamIntegrityPolicy({
   43:           assignmentId:
   44:             assignment.id,
   45:           teacherId,
   46:           policy,
   47:         });
   48: 
   49:       setPolicy(updated);
   50:       onSaved(updated);
   51: 
   52:       toast.success(
   53:         "Exam integrity settings saved.",
   54:       );
   55:     } catch (error) {
   56:       console.error(
   57:         "Unable to save exam integrity settings:",
   58:         error,
   59:       );
   60: 
   61:       toast.error(
   62:         error instanceof Error
   63:           ? error.message
   64:           : "Integrity settings could not be saved.",
   65:       );
   66:     } finally {
   67:       setSaving(false);
   68:     }
   69:   }
   70: 
   71:   return (
   72:     <Card className="overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-indigo-200">
   73:       <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-950 to-violet-900 p-6 text-white">
   74:         <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-indigo-200">
   75:           <ShieldCheck className="h-5 w-5" />
   76:           <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-[0.16em]">
   77:             Exam integrity monitoring
   78:           </p>

   84:

   86:           CS Master records fullscreen and page-visibility events to support
   87:           teacher review. This is integrity monitoring, not a guaranteed
   88:           lockdown browser.
   89:         </p>
   90:       </div>
   91: 
   92:       <div className="grid gap-5 p-6 lg:grid-cols-2">
   93:         <label className="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 p-4">
   94:           <input
   95:             type="checkbox"
   96:             checked={policy.enabled}
   97:             onChange={(event) =>
   98:               setPolicy(
   99:                 (current) => ({
  100:                   ...current,
  101:                   enabled:
  102:                     event.target.checked,
  103:                 }),
  104:               )
  105:             }
  106:             className="mt-1 h-4 w-4"
  107:           />
  108:

  116:             </p>
  117:           </div>
  118:         </label>

FILE: components\teacher\exam-assignments\ExamIntegrityReportCard.tsx
==============================================================================================================
    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import {
    4:   AlertTriangle,
    5:   CheckCircle2,
    6:   ShieldAlert,
    7: } from "lucide-react";
    8: 
    9: import Card from "@/components/ui/Card";
   10: import type {
   11:   ExamIntegrityIncident,
   12:   ExamSubmission,
   13: } from "@/types/examAssignment";
   14: 
   15: function formatDate(
   16:   value: Date | null,
   17: ): string {
   18:   if (!value) {
   19:     return "Time unavailable";
   20:   }
   21: 
   22:   return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(
   23:     "en-GB",
   24:     {
   25:       day: "2-digit",
   26:       month: "short",
   27:       year: "numeric",
   28:       hour: "2-digit",
   29:       minute: "2-digit",
   30:       second: "2-digit",
   31:     },
   32:   ).format(value);
   33: }
   34: 
   35: function incidentLabel(
   36:   incident: ExamIntegrityIncident,
   37: ): string {
   38:   switch (incident.type) {
   39:     case "fullscreen_exit":
   40:       return "Exited fullscreen";
   41: 
   42:     case "fullscreen_restored":
   43:       return "Returned to fullscreen";
   44: 
   45:     case "page_hidden":
   46:       return "Exam page hidden";
   47: 
   48:     case "page_visible":
   49:       return "Exam page visible again";
   50: 
   51:     case "integrity_termination":
   52:       return "Exam terminated";
   53: 
   54:     default:
   55:       return incident.type;
   56:   }
   57: }
   58: 
   59: export default function ExamIntegrityReportCard({
   60:   submission,
   61: }: {
   62:   submission: ExamSubmission;
   63: }) {
   64:   const incidents =
   65:     submission.integrityIncidents || [];
   66: 
   67:   const violationCount =
   68:     incidents.filter(
   69:       (incident) =>
   70:         incident.type ===
   71:           "fullscreen_exit" ||
   72:         incident.type ===
   73:           "page_hidden" ||
   74:         incident.type ===
   75:           "integrity_termination",
   76:     ).length;
   77: 
   78:   return (
   79:     <Card

   81:         submission.integrityTerminated

   84:       }`}
   85:     >
   86:       <div
   87:         className={`p-6 ${
   88:           submission.integrityTerminated
   89:             ? "bg-red-950 text-white"
   90:             : "bg-slate-950 text-white"
   91:         }`}

   93:         <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   94:           <ShieldAlert className="h-5 w-5" />
   95:

FILE: components\teacher\programming\ProgrammingChallengeSelector.tsx
==============================================================================================================
    1: "use client";
    2: 
    3: import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
    4: 
    5: import { allProgrammingChallenges } from "@/data/programming/challenges";
    6: import type {
    7:   ProgrammingChallenge,
    8:   ProgrammingDifficulty,
    9:   ProgrammingExamBoard,
   10:   ProgrammingQualification,
   11: } from "@/types/programming";
   12: 
   13: type Props = {
   14:   selectedChallengeId: string | null;
   15:   onSelect: (challenge: ProgrammingChallenge) => void;
   16: };
   17: 
   18: type ModeFilter = "all" | "practice" | "debug";
   19: 
   20: export default function ProgrammingChallengeSelector({
   21:   selectedChallengeId,
   22:   onSelect,
   23: }: Props) {
   24:   const [qualification, setQualification] =
   25:     useState<ProgrammingQualification>("GCSE");
   26:   const [examBoard, setExamBoard] =
   27:     useState<ProgrammingExamBoard>("AQA");
   28:   const [difficulty, setDifficulty] =
   29:     useState<ProgrammingDifficulty>("foundation");
   30:   const [mode, setMode] = useState<ModeFilter>("all");
   31:   const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
   32: 
   33:   const challenges = useMemo(() => {
   34:     const term = search.trim().toLowerCase();
   35: 
   36:     return allProgrammingChallenges
   37:       .filter((challenge) =>
   38:         challenge.qualifications.includes(qualification),
   39:       )
   40:       .filter(
   41:         (challenge) =>
   42:           !challenge.examBoards ||
   43:           challenge.examBoards.length === 0 ||
   44:           challenge.examBoards.includes(examBoard),
   45:       )
   46:       .filter(
   47:         (challenge) =>
   48:           challenge.difficulty === difficulty,
   49:       )
   50:       .filter(
   51:         (challenge) =>
   52:           mode === "all" || challenge.mode === mode,
   53:       )
   54:       .filter(
   55:         (challenge) =>
   56:           !term ||
   57:           challenge.title.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
   58:           challenge.description
   59:             .toLowerCase()
   60:             .includes(term) ||
   61:           challenge.topicId
   62:             .toLowerCase()
   63:             .includes(term) ||
   64:           challenge.skills.some((skill) =>
   65:             skill.toLowerCase().includes(term),
   66:           ),
   67:       )
   68:       .sort((a, b) =>
   69:         a.title.localeCompare(b.title),
   70:       );
   71:   }, [
   72:     difficulty,
   73:     examBoard,
   74:     mode,
   75:     qualification,
   76:     search,
   77:   ]);
   78: 
   79:   return (
   80:     <div className="mt-8 rounded-2xl border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50/40 p-6">
   81:       <p className="text-sm font-black uppercase tracking-wide text-blue-700">
   82:         Programming challenge library
   83:       </p>
   84: 
   85:       <h3 className="mt-2 text-xl font-black text-slate-950">
   86:         Choose the exact challenge
   87:       </h3>
   88: 
   89:       <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-600">
   90:         The selected challenge is locked into the assignment. Students
   91:         will open this exact task rather than receiving an adaptive
   92:         challenge.
   93:       </p>
   94: 
   95:       <div className="mt-5 grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-5">
   96:         <Select
   97:           label="Qualification"
   98:           value={qualification}
   99:           onChange={(value) =>
  100:             setQualification(
  101:               value as ProgrammingQualification,
  102:             )
  103:           }
  104:           options={[
  105:             ["GCSE", "GCSE"],
  106:             ["A_LEVEL", "A-Level"],
  107:           ]}
  108:         />
  109: 
  110:         <Select
  111:           label="Exam board"
  112:           value={examBoard}
  113:           onChange={(value) =>
  114:             setExamBoard(
  115:               value as ProgrammingExamBoard,
  116:             )
  117:           }
  118:           options={[
  119:             ["AQA", "AQA"],
  120:             ["OCR", "OCR"],
  121:             ["EDEXCEL", "Edexcel"],
  122:           ]}
  123:         />
  124: 
  125:         <Select
  126:           label="Difficulty"
  127:           value={difficulty}
  128:           onChange={(value) =>
  129:             setDifficulty(
  130:               value as ProgrammingDifficulty,
  131:             )
  132:           }
  133:           options={[
  134:             ["foundation", "Foundation"],
  135:             ["intermediate", "Intermediate"],
  136:             ["higher", "Higher"],
  137:           ]}
  138:         />
  139: 
  140:         <Select
  141:           label="Mode"
  142:           value={mode}
  143:           onChange={(value) =>
  144:             setMode(value as ModeFilter)
  145:           }
  146:           options={[
  147:             ["all", "All"],
  148:             ["practice", "Practice"],
  149:             ["debug", "Debug"],
  150:           ]}
  151:         />
  152: 
  153:         <label className="block">
  154:           <span className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
  155:             Search
  156:           </span>
  157: 
  158:           <input
  159:             type="search"
  160:             value={search}
  161:             onChange={(event) =>
  162:               setSearch(event.target.value)
  163:             }
  164:             placeholder="Title, topic, skill..."
  165:             className="mt-2 min-h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
  166:           />
  167:         </label>
  168:       </div>
  169: 
  170:       {challenges.length === 0 ? (
  171:         <div className="mt-6 rounded-2xl bg-white p-6 text-center text-slate-600">
  172:           No programming challenges match these filters.
  173:         </div>
  174:       ) : (
  175:         <div className="mt-6 grid gap-4 xl:grid-cols-2">
  176:           {challenges.map((challenge) => {
  177:             const selected =
  178:               selectedChallengeId === challenge.id;
  179: 
  180:             return (
  181:               <button
  182:                 key={challenge.id}
  183:                 type="button"
  184:                 onClick={() => onSelect(challenge)}
  185:                 className={`rounded-2xl border p-5 text-left transition ${
  186:                   selected
  187:                     ? "border-blue-600 bg-white ring-2 ring-blue-100"
  188:                     : "border-blue-100 bg-white hover:border-blue-300"
  189:                 }`}
  190:               >
  191:                 <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
  192:                   <div>
  193:                     <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wide text-blue-600">
  194:                       {challenge.mode} Â·{" "}
  195:                       {challenge.difficulty}
  196:                     </p>
  197: 
  198:                     <h4 className="mt-2 font-black text-slate-950">
  199:                       {challenge.title}
  200:                     </h4>
  201:                   </div>
  202: 
  203:                   <span
  204:                     className={`flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full border font-black ${
  205:                       selected
  206:                         ? "border-blue-600 bg-blue-600 text-white"
  207:                         : "border-slate-300 text-transparent"
  208:                     }`}
  209:                   >
  210:                     âœ“
  211:                   </span>
  212:                 </div>
  213: 
  214:                 <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-600">
  215:                   {challenge.description}
  216:                 </p>
  217: 
  218:                 <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-xs font-bold">
  219:                   <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-3 py-1 text-slate-700">
  220:                     {challenge.topicId}
  221:                   </span>
  222: 
  223:                   <span className="rounded-full bg-emerald-50 px-3 py-1 text-emerald-700">
  224:                     {challenge.xpReward} XP
  225:                   </span>
  226: 
  227:                   <span className="rounded-full bg-amber-50 px-3 py-1 text-amber-700">
  228:                     ~{challenge.estimatedMinutes} min
  229:                   </span>
  230: 
  231:                   {challenge.skills
  232:                     .slice(0, 4)
  233:                     .map((skill) => (
  234:                       <span
  235:                         key={skill}
  236:                         className="rounded-full bg-blue-50 px-3 py-1 text-blue-700"
  237:                       >
  238:                         {skill}
  239:                       </span>
  240:                     ))}
  241:                 </div>
  242:               </button>
  243:             );
  244:           })}
  245:         </div>
  246:       )}
  247:     </div>
  248:   );
  249: }
  250: 
  251: function Select({
  252:   label,
  253:   value,
  254:   onChange,
  255:   options,
  256: }: {
  257:   label: string;
  258:   value: string;
  259:   onChange: (value: string) => void;
  260:   options: Array<[string, string]>;
  261: }) {
  262:   return (
  263:     <label className="block">
  264:       <span className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
  265:         {label}
  266:       </span>
  267: 
  268:       <select
  269:         value={value}
  270:         onChange={(event) =>
  271:           onChange(event.target.value)
  272:         }
  273:         className="mt-2 min-h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 text-sm font-semibold outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
  274:       >
  275:         {options.map(
  276:           ([optionValue, optionLabel]) => (
  277:             <option
  278:               key={optionValue}
  279:               value={optionValue}
  280:             >
  281:               {optionLabel}
  282:             </option>
  283:           ),
  284:         )}
  285:       </select>
  286:     </label>
  287:   );
  288: }

    1: import {
    2:   doc,
    3:   serverTimestamp,
    4:   setDoc,
    5: } from "firebase/firestore";

    7: import { db } from "@/lib/firebase";
    8: 
    9: export type QuizIntegrityIncidentType =
   10:   | "fullscreen_exit"
   11:   | "fullscreen_restored"
   12:   | "page_hidden"
   13:   | "page_visible"
   14:   | "auto_submit";
   15: 
   16: export type QuizIntegrityIncident = {
   17:   id: string;
   18:   type: QuizIntegrityIncidentType;
   19:   occurredAt: string;
   20:   questionNumber: number | null;
   21:   detail: string;
   22: };
   23: 
   24: export type SaveAssignmentResultInput = {
   25:   assignmentId: string;
   26:   studentId: string;
   27:   classId: string;
   28:   teacherId: string;
   29:   assignmentType: "lesson" | "quiz";
   30:   resourceId: string;
   31: 
   32:   score?: number;
   33:   totalQuestions?: number;
   34:   percentage?: number;
   35:   earnedXP?: number;
   36:   timeTakenSeconds?: number;
   37: 
   38:   /*
   39:    * Quiz delivery / integrity evidence.
   40:    *
   41:    * Practice assignments keep the default "practice" value.
   42:    * Assessment quizzes may additionally save an integrity timeline.
   43:    */
   44:   deliveryMode?: "practice" | "assessment";
   45:   integritySessionStartedAt?: string | null;
   46:   integrityIncidents?: QuizIntegrityIncident[];
   47:   integrityTerminated?: boolean;
   48:   integrityTerminationReason?: string;
   49: };
   50: 
   51: export async function saveAssignmentResult({
   52:   assignmentId,
   53:   studentId,
   54:   classId,
   55:   teacherId,
   56:   assignmentType,
   57:   resourceId,
   58:   score = 0,
   59:   totalQuestions = 0,
   60:   percentage = 0,
   61:   earnedXP = 0,
   62:   timeTakenSeconds = 0,
   63:   deliveryMode = "practice",
   64:   integritySessionStartedAt = null,
   65:   integrityIncidents = [],
   66:   integrityTerminated = false,
   67:   integrityTerminationReason = "",
   68: }: SaveAssignmentResultInput) {
   69:   const resultId = `${assignmentId}_${studentId}`;
   70: 
   71:   const resultRef = doc(
   72:     db,
   73:     "assignmentResults",
   74:     resultId,
   75:   );
   76: 
   77:   await setDoc(
   78:     resultRef,
   79:     {
   80:       assignmentId,
   81:       studentId,
   82:       classId,
   83:       teacherId,
   84:       assignmentType,
   85:       resourceId,
   86: 
   87:       score,
   88:       totalQuestions,
   89:       percentage,
   90:       earnedXP,
   91:       timeTakenSeconds,
   92: 
   93:       status: "completed",
   94: 
   95:       deliveryMode,
   96: 
   97:       ...(deliveryMode === "assessment"
   98:         ? {
   99:             integritySessionStartedAt,
  100:             integrityIncidents,
  101:             integrityTerminated,
  102:             integrityTerminationReason,
  103:           }
  104:         : {}),
  105: 
  106:       completedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  107:     },
  108:     {
  109:       merge: true,
  110:     },
  111:   );
  112: 
  113:   return resultId;
  114: }

FILE: services\assignmentService.ts

    1: import {
    2:   addDoc,
    3:   arrayUnion,
    4:   collection,
    5:   doc,
    6:   serverTimestamp,
    7:   updateDoc,
    8: } from "firebase/firestore";
    9: 
   10: import { db } from "@/lib/firebase";
   11: 
   12: export type QuizAssignmentSource =
   13:   | "built-in"
   14:   | "ai-generated";
   15: 
   16: export type QuizDeliveryMode =
   17:   | "practice"
   18:   | "assessment";
   19: 
   20: export type CreateAssignmentInput = {
   21:   teacherId: string;
   22:   classId: string;
   23:   title: string;
   24:   description: string;
   25:   type: "lesson" | "quiz";
   26:   resourceId: string;
   27:   dueDate: string;
   28: 
   29:   quizSource?: QuizAssignmentSource;
   30:   deliveryMode?: QuizDeliveryMode;
   31: };
   32: 
   33: export async function createAssignment({
   34:   teacherId,
   35:   classId,
   36:   title,
   37:   description,
   38:   type,
   39:   resourceId,
   40:   dueDate,
   41:   quizSource,
   42:   deliveryMode,
   43: }: CreateAssignmentInput) {
   44:   const assignmentRef = await addDoc(
   45:     collection(db, "assignments"),
   46:     {
   47:       teacherId,
   48:       classId,
   49:       title,
   50:       description,
   51:       type,
   52:       resourceId,
   53:       dueDate,
   54: 
   55:       ...(type === "quiz"
   56:         ? {
   57:             quizSource:
   58:               quizSource || "built-in",
   59: 
   60:             deliveryMode:
   61:               deliveryMode || "practice",
   62:           }
   63:         : {}),
   64: 
   65:       status: "active",
   66:       createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
   67:     },
   68:   );
   69: 
   70:   await updateDoc(
   71:     doc(db, "classes", classId),
   72:     {
   73:       assignmentIds: arrayUnion(
   74:         assignmentRef.id,
   75:       ),
   76:     },
   77:   );
   78: 
   79:   return assignmentRef.id;
   80: }

==============================================================================================================
FILE: services\examAssignmentService.ts

    1: import {
    2:   addDoc,
    3:   collection,
    4:   deleteDoc,
    5:   doc,
    6:   getDoc,
    7:   getDocs,
    8:   orderBy,
    9:   query,
   10:   serverTimestamp,
   11:   Timestamp,
   12:   updateDoc,
   13:   where,
   14: } from "firebase/firestore";
   15: 
   16: import { db } from "@/lib/firebase";
   17: import {
   18:   DEFAULT_EXAM_INTEGRITY_POLICY,
   19:   type CreateExamAssignmentInput,
   20:   type ExamAssignment,
   21:   type ExamAssignmentStatus,
   22:   type ExamIntegrityPolicy,
   23:   type ExamVisibilityAction,
   24: } from "@/types/examAssignment";
   25: 
   26: type FirestoreExamAssignment = Omit<
   27:   ExamAssignment,
   28:   "id" | "dueDate" | "createdAt" | "updatedAt"
   29: > & {
   30:   dueDate?: Timestamp;
   31:   createdAt?: Timestamp;
   32:   updatedAt?: Timestamp;
   33: };
   34: 
   35: function toDate(value?: Timestamp | null): Date | null {
   36:   return value?.toDate ? value.toDate() : null;
   37: }
   38: 
   39: function visibilityAction(value: unknown): ExamVisibilityAction {
   40:   return value === "pause" || value === "auto_submit"
   41:     ? value
   42:     : "warn";
   43: }
   44: 
   45: function normaliseIntegrityPolicy(
   46:   value?: Partial<ExamIntegrityPolicy> | null,
   47: ): ExamIntegrityPolicy {
   48:   return {
   49:     enabled:
   50:       typeof value?.enabled === "boolean"
   51:         ? value.enabled
   52:         : DEFAULT_EXAM_INTEGRITY_POLICY.enabled,
   53: 
   54:     fullscreenRequired:
   55:       typeof value?.fullscreenRequired === "boolean"
   56:         ? value.fullscreenRequired
   57:         : DEFAULT_EXAM_INTEGRITY_POLICY.fullscreenRequired,
   58: 
   59:     /*
   60:      * The five-second fullscreen rule is deliberately fixed.
   61:      */
   62:     fullscreenExitCountdownSeconds: 5,
   63: 
   64:     visibilityAction: visibilityAction(
   65:       value?.visibilityAction,
   66:     ),
   67: 
   68:     monitorPageVisibility:
   69:       typeof value?.monitorPageVisibility === "boolean"
   70:         ? value.monitorPageVisibility
   71:         : DEFAULT_EXAM_INTEGRITY_POLICY.monitorPageVisibility,
   72:   };
   73: }
   74: 
   75: function convertAssignment(
   76:   id: string,
   77:   data: FirestoreExamAssignment,
   78: ): ExamAssignment {
   79:   return {
   80:     id,
   81:     teacherId: data.teacherId,
   82:     teacherName: data.teacherName || "Teacher",
   83:     classId: data.classId,
   84:     className: data.className,
   85:     studentIds: data.studentIds || [],
   86:     questionSetId: data.questionSetId,
   87:     questionSetTitle: data.questionSetTitle,
   88:     questionSetSnapshot: data.questionSetSnapshot,
   89:     title: data.title,
   90:     instructions: data.instructions || "",
   91:     dueDate: toDate(data.dueDate),
   92:     status: data.status || "active",
   93:     totalMarks: data.totalMarks || 0,
   94:     questionCount: data.questionCount || 0,
   95:     submittedCount: data.submittedCount || 0,
   96:     markedCount: data.markedCount || 0,
   97:     integrityPolicy: normaliseIntegrityPolicy(
   98:       data.integrityPolicy,
   99:     ),
  100:     createdAt: toDate(data.createdAt),
  101:     updatedAt: toDate(data.updatedAt),
  102:   };
  103: }
  104: 
  105: function validateInput(input: CreateExamAssignmentInput) {
  106:   if (!input.teacherId.trim()) {
  107:     throw new Error("A teacher account is required.");
  108:   }
  109: 
  110:   if (!input.classId.trim()) {
  111:     throw new Error("Select a class.");
  112:   }
  113: 
  114:   if (!input.questionSetId.trim()) {
  115:     throw new Error("A question set is required.");
  116:   }
  117: 
  118:   if (!input.title.trim()) {
  119:     throw new Error("Enter an assignment title.");
  120:   }
  121: 
  122:   if (
  123:     !(input.dueDate instanceof Date) ||
  124:     Number.isNaN(input.dueDate.getTime())
  125:   ) {
  126:     throw new Error("Select a valid due date.");
  127:   }
  128: 
  129:   if (input.studentIds.length === 0) {
  130:     throw new Error(
  131:       "The selected class has no enrolled students.",
  132:     );
  133:   }
  134: }
  135: 
  136: function sameCalendarDay(
  137:   first: Date | null,
  138:   second: Date,
  139: ): boolean {
  140:   if (!first) {
  141:     return false;
  142:   }
  143: 
  144:   return (
  145:     first.getFullYear() === second.getFullYear() &&
  146:     first.getMonth() === second.getMonth() &&
  147:     first.getDate() === second.getDate()
  148:   );
  149: }
  150: 
  151: async function findExistingExamAssignment(
  152:   input: CreateExamAssignmentInput,
  153: ): Promise<ExamAssignment | null> {
  154:   const snapshot = await getDocs(
  155:     query(
  156:       collection(db, "examAssignments"),
  157:       where(
  158:         "teacherId",
  159:         "==",
  160:         input.teacherId.trim(),
  161:       ),
  162:     ),
  163:   );
  164: 
  165:   const assignments = snapshot.docs.map(
  166:     (document) =>
  167:       convertAssignment(
  168:         document.id,
  169:         document.data() as FirestoreExamAssignment,
  170:       ),
  171:   );
  172: 
  173:   return (
  174:     assignments.find(
  175:       (assignment) =>
  176:         assignment.status === "active" &&
  177:         assignment.classId === input.classId.trim() &&
  178:         assignment.questionSetId ===
  179:           input.questionSetId.trim() &&
  180:         sameCalendarDay(
  181:           assignment.dueDate,
  182:           input.dueDate,
  183:         ),
  184:     ) || null
  185:   );
  186: }
  187: 
  188: export async function createExamAssignment(
  189:   input: CreateExamAssignmentInput,
  190: ): Promise<string> {
  191:   validateInput(input);
  192: 
  193:   const existing =
  194:     await findExistingExamAssignment(input);
  195: 
  196:   if (existing) {
  197:     throw new Error(
  198:       "This paper is already assigned to that class with the same due date.",
  199:     );
  200:   }
  201: 
  202:   const studentIds = Array.from(
  203:     new Set(
  204:       input.studentIds
  205:         .map((id) => id.trim())
  206:         .filter(Boolean),
  207:     ),
  208:   );
  209: 
  210:   const reference = await addDoc(
  211:     collection(db, "examAssignments"),
  212:     {
  213:       teacherId: input.teacherId.trim(),
  214:       teacherName:
  215:         input.teacherName?.trim() || "Teacher",
  216:       classId: input.classId.trim(),
  217:       className: input.className.trim(),
  218:       studentIds,
  219:       questionSetId: input.questionSetId.trim(),
  220:       questionSetTitle:
  221:         input.questionSetTitle.trim(),
  222:       questionSetSnapshot:
  223:         input.questionSetSnapshot,
  224:       title: input.title.trim(),
  225:       instructions:
  226:         input.instructions?.trim() || "",
  227:       dueDate: Timestamp.fromDate(input.dueDate),
  228:       status: "active",
  229:       totalMarks:
  230:         input.questionSetSnapshot.totalMarks,
  231:       questionCount:
  232:         input.questionSetSnapshot.questionCount,
  233:       submittedCount: 0,
  234:       markedCount: 0,
  235:       integrityPolicy:
  236:         normaliseIntegrityPolicy(
  237:           input.integrityPolicy,
  238:         ),
  239:       createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
  240:       updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  241:     },
  242:   );
  243: 
  244:   return reference.id;
  245: }
  246: 
  247: export async function getExamAssignmentById(
  248:   assignmentId: string,
  249: ): Promise<ExamAssignment | null> {
  250:   if (!assignmentId.trim()) {
  251:     return null;
  252:   }
  253: 
  254:   const snapshot = await getDoc(
  255:     doc(
  256:       db,
  257:       "examAssignments",
  258:       assignmentId,
  259:     ),
  260:   );
  261: 
  262:   if (!snapshot.exists()) {
  263:     return null;
  264:   }
  265: 
  266:   return convertAssignment(
  267:     snapshot.id,
  268:     snapshot.data() as FirestoreExamAssignment,
  269:   );
  270: }
  271: 
  272: export async function getTeacherExamAssignments(
  273:   teacherId: string,
  274: ): Promise<ExamAssignment[]> {
  275:   if (!teacherId.trim()) {
  276:     return [];
  277:   }
  278: 
  279:   const snapshot = await getDocs(
  280:     query(
  281:       collection(db, "examAssignments"),
  282:       where("teacherId", "==", teacherId),
  283:       orderBy("createdAt", "desc"),
  284:     ),
  285:   );
  286: 
  287:   return snapshot.docs.map(
  288:     (document) =>
  289:       convertAssignment(
  290:         document.id,
  291:         document.data() as FirestoreExamAssignment,
  292:       ),
  293:   );
  294: }
  295: 
  296: export async function getStudentExamAssignments(
  297:   studentId: string,
  298: ): Promise<ExamAssignment[]> {
  299:   if (!studentId.trim()) {
  300:     return [];
  301:   }
  302: 
  303:   const snapshot = await getDocs(
  304:     query(
  305:       collection(db, "examAssignments"),
  306:       where(
  307:         "studentIds",
  308:         "array-contains",
  309:         studentId,
  310:       ),
  311:       orderBy("dueDate", "asc"),
  312:     ),
  313:   );
  314: 
  315:   return snapshot.docs.map(
  316:     (document) =>
  317:       convertAssignment(
  318:         document.id,
  319:         document.data() as FirestoreExamAssignment,
  320:       ),
  321:   );
  322: }
  323: 
  324: export async function updateExamAssignmentStatus(
  325:   assignmentId: string,
  326:   status: ExamAssignmentStatus,
  327: ): Promise<void> {
  328:   await updateDoc(
  329:     doc(
  330:       db,
  331:       "examAssignments",
  332:       assignmentId,
  333:     ),
  334:     {
  335:       status,
  336:       updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  337:     },
  338:   );
  339: }

  341: export async function updateExamIntegrityPolicy({
  342:   assignmentId,
  343:   teacherId,
  344:   policy,
  345: }: {
  346:   assignmentId: string;
  347:   teacherId: string;
  348:   policy: ExamIntegrityPolicy;
  349: }): Promise<ExamIntegrityPolicy> {
  350:   const reference = doc(
  351:     db,
  352:     "examAssignments",
  353:     assignmentId,
  354:   );
  355: 
  356:   const snapshot = await getDoc(reference);
  357: 
  358:   if (!snapshot.exists()) {
  359:     throw new Error(
  360:       "Exam assignment not found.",
  361:     );
  362:   }
  363: 
  364:   if (
  365:     snapshot.data().teacherId !==
  366:     teacherId.trim()
  367:   ) {
  368:     throw new Error(
  369:       "You do not have permission to change this exam.",
  370:     );
  371:   }
  372: 
  373:   const cleaned =
  374:     normaliseIntegrityPolicy(policy);
  375: 
  376:   await updateDoc(reference, {
  377:     integrityPolicy: cleaned,
  378:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  379:   });
  380: 
  381:   return cleaned;
  382: }

==============================================================================================================
FILE: services\lessonAssignmentService.tsx
==============================================================================================================
    1: import { topicLibrary } from "@/data/curriculum/topics";
    2: import type { Lesson, Topic } from "@/types/curriculum";
    3: 
    4: const LESSON_RESOURCE_SEPARATOR = "::";
    5: 
    6: export type LessonAssignmentIdentity = {
    7:   topicId: string;
    8:   lessonId: string;
    9: };
   10: 
   11: export type LessonAssignmentOption = {
   12:   topicId: string;
   13:   topicTitle: string;
   14:   lesson: Lesson;
   15: };
   16: 
   17: export function createLessonAssignmentResourceId(
   18:   topicId: string,
   19:   lessonId: string,
   20: ): string {
   21:   return `${topicId}${LESSON_RESOURCE_SEPARATOR}${lessonId}`;
   22: }
   23: 
   24: export function parseLessonAssignmentResourceId(
   25:   resourceId: string,
   26: ): LessonAssignmentIdentity | null {
   27:   const separatorIndex = resourceId.indexOf(
   28:     LESSON_RESOURCE_SEPARATOR,
   29:   );
   30: 
   31:   if (separatorIndex <= 0) {
   32:     return null;
   33:   }
   34: 
   35:   const topicId = resourceId.slice(0, separatorIndex).trim();
   36:   const lessonId = resourceId
   37:     .slice(separatorIndex + LESSON_RESOURCE_SEPARATOR.length)
   38:     .trim();
   39: 
   40:   if (!topicId || !lessonId) {
   41:     return null;
   42:   }
   43: 
   44:   return { topicId, lessonId };
   45: }
   46: 
   47: export function getLessonAssignmentOption(
   48:   resourceId: string,
   49: ): LessonAssignmentOption | null {
   50:   const identity = parseLessonAssignmentResourceId(resourceId);
   51: 
   52:   if (!identity) {
   53:     return null;
   54:   }
   55: 
   56:   const topic = (topicLibrary as Record<string, Topic>)[identity.topicId];
   57: 
   58:   if (!topic) {
   59:     return null;
   60:   }
   61: 
   62:   const lesson = topic.lessons.find(
   63:     (item) => item.id === identity.lessonId,
   64:   );
   65: 
   66:   if (!lesson) {
   67:     return null;
   68:   }
   69: 
   70:   return {
   71:     topicId: topic.id,
   72:     topicTitle: topic.title,
   73:     lesson,
   74:   };
   75: }
   76: 
   77: export function getLessonAssignmentHref(
   78:   resourceId: string,
   79:   assignmentId?: string,
   80: ): string | null {
   81:   const identity = parseLessonAssignmentResourceId(resourceId);
   82: 
   83:   if (!identity) {
   84:     return null;
   85:   }
   86: 
   87:   const search = new URLSearchParams({
   88:     lesson: identity.lessonId,
   89:   });
   90: 
   91:   if (assignmentId) {
   92:     search.set("assignment", assignmentId);
   93:   }
   94: 
   95:   return `/learn/${encodeURIComponent(identity.topicId)}?${search.toString()}`;
   96: }

   32: function toDate(value: unknown): Date | null {
   33:   if (!value) return null;
   34:   if (value instanceof Date) return value;
   35: 
   36:   if (
   37:     typeof value === "object" &&
   38:     value !== null &&
   39:     "toDate" in value &&
   40:     typeof (value as { toDate?: unknown }).toDate === "function"
   41:   ) {
   42:     return (value as { toDate: () => Date }).toDate();
   43:   }
   44: 
   45:   return null;
   46: }

   48: function progressId(assignmentId: string, studentId: string): string {
   49:   return `${assignmentId}_${studentId}`;

   52: function toChallengeSnapshot(
   53:   challengeId: string,
   54: ): ProgrammingChallengeSnapshot {
   55:   const challenge = getProgrammingChallengeById(challengeId);
   56: 
   57:   if (!challenge) {
   58:     throw new Error(
   59:       "The selected programming challenge could not be found.",
   60:     );
   61:   }
   62: 
   63:   return {
   64:     challengeId: challenge.id,
   65:     title: challenge.title,
   66:     topicId: challenge.topicId,
   67:     skills: challenge.skills,
   68:     mode: challenge.mode,
   69:     difficulty: challenge.difficulty,
   70:     qualifications: challenge.qualifications,
   71:     examBoards: challenge.examBoards ?? [],
   72:     xpReward: challenge.xpReward,
   73:     estimatedMinutes: challenge.estimatedMinutes,
   74:   };

   77: function convertAssignment(
   78:   id: string,
   79:   data: Record<string, unknown>,
   80: ): ProgrammingAssignment {
   81:   const storedSnapshot =
   82:     (data.programmingChallenge as
   83:       | ProgrammingChallengeSnapshot
   84:       | undefined) ?? null;
   85: 
   86:   const challengeId = String(
   87:     data.resourceId ?? storedSnapshot?.challengeId ?? "",
   88:   );
   89: 
   90:   const challengeSnapshot =
   91:     storedSnapshot ?? toChallengeSnapshot(challengeId);
   92: 
   93:   return {
   94:     id,
   95:     teacherId: String(data.teacherId ?? ""),
   96:     teacherName: String(data.teacherName ?? "Teacher"),
   97:     classId: String(data.classId ?? ""),
   98:     className: String(data.className ?? "Class"),
   99:     studentIds: Array.isArray(data.studentIds)
  100:       ? data.studentIds.filter(
  101:           (value): value is string => typeof value === "string",
  102:         )
  103:       : [],
  104:     studentCount:
  105:       typeof data.studentCount === "number" ? data.studentCount : 0,
  106:     completedCount:
  107:       typeof data.completedCount === "number" ? data.completedCount : 0,
  108:     challengeId,
  109:     title: String(data.resourceTitle ?? challengeSnapshot.title),
  110:     topic: String(data.resourceTopic ?? challengeSnapshot.topicId),
  111:     instructions: String(data.instructions ?? ""),
  112:     dueDate: toDate(data.dueDate),
  113:     createdAt: toDate(data.createdAt),
  114:     status: data.status === "archived" ? "archived" : "active",
  115:     challengeSnapshot,
  116:   };
  117: }

  119: export type CreateProgrammingAssignmentInput = {
  120:   teacherId: string;
  121:   classId: string;
  122:   challengeId: string;
  123:   instructions: string;
  124:   dueDate: string;
  125: };
  126: 
  127: export async function createProgrammingAssignment({
  128:   teacherId,
  129:   classId,
  130:   challengeId,
  131:   instructions,
  132:   dueDate,
  133: }: CreateProgrammingAssignmentInput): Promise<string> {
  134:   const cleanedTeacherId = teacherId.trim();
  135:   const cleanedClassId = classId.trim();
  136:   const cleanedChallengeId = challengeId.trim();
  137: 
  138:   if (!cleanedTeacherId || !cleanedClassId || !cleanedChallengeId) {
  139:     throw new Error(
  140:       "Teacher, class and programming challenge are required.",
  141:     );
  142:   }
  143: 
  144:   const parsedDueDate = new Date(`${dueDate}T23:59:59`);
  145: 
  146:   if (Number.isNaN(parsedDueDate.getTime())) {
  147:     throw new Error("Select a valid due date.");
  148:   }
  149: 
  150:   const challenge = getProgrammingChallengeById(cleanedChallengeId);
  151: 
  152:   if (!challenge) {
  153:     throw new Error(
  154:       "The selected programming challenge could not be found.",
  155:     );
  156:   }
  157: 
  158:   const [classSnapshot, teacherSnapshot] = await Promise.all([
  159:     getDoc(doc(db, "classes", cleanedClassId)),
  160:     getDoc(doc(db, "users", cleanedTeacherId)),
  161:   ]);
  162: 
  163:   if (!classSnapshot.exists()) {
  164:     throw new Error("The selected class could not be found.");
  165:   }
  166: 
  167:   const classData = classSnapshot.data();
  168:   const teacherData = teacherSnapshot.exists()
  169:     ? teacherSnapshot.data()
  170:     : {};
  171: 
  172:   if (
  173:     classData.teacherId &&
  174:     classData.teacherId !== cleanedTeacherId
  175:   ) {
  176:     throw new Error(
  177:       "You cannot assign work to another teacher's class.",
  178:     );
  179:   }
  180: 
  181:   const studentIds = Array.isArray(classData.studentIds)
  182:     ? Array.from(
  183:         new Set(
  184:           classData.studentIds.filter(
  185:             (value: unknown): value is string =>
  186:               typeof value === "string" && Boolean(value.trim()),
  187:           ),
  188:         ),
  189:       )
  190:     : [];
  191: 
  192:   const teacherName = String(
  193:     teacherData.name ??
  194:       teacherData.displayName ??
  195:       teacherData.fullName ??
  196:       teacherData.email ??
  197:       "Teacher",
  198:   );
  199: 
  200:   const challengeSnapshot =
  201:     toChallengeSnapshot(challenge.id);
  202: 
  203:   const assignmentReference = await addDoc(
  204:     collection(db, "classAssignments"),
  205:     {
  206:       resourceId: challenge.id,
  207:       resourceTitle: challenge.title,
  208:       resourceTopic: challenge.topicId,
  209:       resourceType: PROGRAMMING_RESOURCE_TYPE,
  210: 
  211:       teacherId: cleanedTeacherId,
  212:       teacherName,
  213: 
  214:       classId: cleanedClassId,
  215:       className: String(classData.name ?? "Untitled Class"),
  216: 
  217:       instructions:
  218:         instructions.trim() ||
  219:         `Complete the ${challenge.title} programming challenge.`,
  220: 
  221:       dueDate: Timestamp.fromDate(parsedDueDate),
  222:       status: "active",
  223: 
  224:       studentIds,
  225:       studentCount: studentIds.length,
  226:       completedCount: 0,
  227: 
  228:       programmingChallenge: challengeSnapshot,
  229: 
  230:       createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
  231:       updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  232:     },
  233:   );
  234: 
  235:   await updateDoc(doc(db, "classes", cleanedClassId), {
  236:     assignmentIds: arrayUnion(assignmentReference.id),
  237:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  238:   });
  239: 
  240:   return assignmentReference.id;
  241: }
  242: 
  243: export async function getProgrammingAssignmentById(
  244:   assignmentId: string,
  245: ): Promise<ProgrammingAssignment | null> {
  246:   const cleanedId = assignmentId.trim();
  247:   if (!cleanedId) return null;
  248: 
  249:   const snapshot = await getDoc(
  250:     doc(db, "classAssignments", cleanedId),
  251:   );
  252: 
  253:   if (!snapshot.exists()) return null;
  254: 
  255:   const data = snapshot.data() as Record<string, unknown>;
  256: 
  257:   if (data.resourceType !== PROGRAMMING_RESOURCE_TYPE) {
  258:     return null;
  259:   }
  260: 
  261:   return convertAssignment(snapshot.id, data);
  262: }
  263: 
  264: export async function getTeacherProgrammingAssignments(
  265:   teacherId: string,
  266: ): Promise<ProgrammingAssignment[]> {
  267:   if (!teacherId.trim()) return [];
  268: 
  269:   const snapshot = await getDocs(
  270:     query(
  271:       collection(db, "classAssignments"),
  272:       where("teacherId", "==", teacherId),
  273:     ),
  274:   );
  275: 
  276:   return snapshot.docs
  277:     .filter(
  278:       (item) =>
  279:         item.data().resourceType === PROGRAMMING_RESOURCE_TYPE,
  280:     )
  281:     .map((item) =>
  282:       convertAssignment(
  283:         item.id,
  284:         item.data() as Record<string, unknown>,
  285:       ),
  286:     )
  287:     .sort(
  288:       (a, b) =>
  289:         (b.createdAt?.getTime() ?? 0) -
  290:         (a.createdAt?.getTime() ?? 0),
  291:     );
  292: }

  294: export async function getProgrammingSubmission(
  295:   assignmentId: string,
  296:   studentId: string,
  297: ): Promise<ProgrammingSubmission | null> {
  298:   if (!assignmentId.trim() || !studentId.trim()) {
  299:     return null;
  300:   }
  301: 
  302:   const snapshot = await getDoc(
  303:     doc(
  304:       db,
  305:       "assignmentProgress",
  306:       progressId(assignmentId, studentId),
  307:     ),
  308:   );
  309: 
  310:   if (!snapshot.exists()) return null;
  311: 
  312:   const data = snapshot.data();
  313: 
  314:   return {
  315:     id: snapshot.id,
  316:     assignmentId,
  317:     studentId,
  318:     studentName: String(data.studentName ?? "Student"),
  319:     status:
  320:       data.status === "completed"
  321:         ? "completed"
  322:         : data.status === "in_progress"
  323:           ? "in_progress"
  324:           : "not_started",
  325:     attempts:
  326:       typeof data.programmingAttempts === "number"
  327:         ? data.programmingAttempts
  328:         : 0,
  329:     code: String(data.programmingCode ?? ""),
  330:     passedCount:
  331:       typeof data.programmingPassedCount === "number"
  332:         ? data.programmingPassedCount
  333:         : 0,
  334:     totalTests:
  335:       typeof data.programmingTotalTests === "number"
  336:         ? data.programmingTotalTests
  337:         : 0,
  338:     percentage:
  339:       typeof data.programmingPercentage === "number"
  340:         ? data.programmingPercentage
  341:         : 0,
  342:     lastPassed: data.programmingLastPassed === true,
  343:     lastError: String(data.programmingLastError ?? ""),
  344:     startedAt: toDate(data.startedAt),
  345:     completedAt: toDate(data.completedAt),
  346:     updatedAt: toDate(data.updatedAt),
  347:   };
  348: }
  349: 
  350: export type RecordProgrammingAttemptInput = {
  351:   assignmentId: string;
  352:   studentId: string;
  353:   code: string;
  354:   passed: boolean;
  355:   passedCount: number;
  356:   totalTests: number;
  357:   error?: string;
  358: };
  359: 
  360: export async function recordProgrammingAssignmentAttempt({
  361:   assignmentId,
  362:   studentId,
  363:   code,
  364:   passed,
  365:   passedCount,
  366:   totalTests,
  367:   error = "",
  368: }: RecordProgrammingAttemptInput): Promise<void> {
  369:   const assignment =
  370:     await getProgrammingAssignmentById(assignmentId);
  371: 
  372:   if (!assignment) {
  373:     throw new Error(
  374:       "The programming assignment could not be found.",
  375:     );
  376:   }
  377: 
  378:   if (!assignment.studentIds.includes(studentId)) {
  379:     throw new Error(
  380:       "You are not enrolled in this programming assignment.",
  381:     );
  382:   }
  383: 
  384:   const id = progressId(assignmentId, studentId);
  385:   const progressReference = doc(
  386:     db,
  387:     "assignmentProgress",
  388:     id,
  389:   );
  390:   const existing = await getDoc(progressReference);
  391: 
  392:   const previousAttempts =
  393:     existing.exists() &&
  394:     typeof existing.data().programmingAttempts === "number"
  395:       ? existing.data().programmingAttempts
  396:       : 0;
  397: 
  398:   const studentSnapshot = await getDoc(
  399:     doc(db, "users", studentId),
  400:   );
  401:   const studentData = studentSnapshot.exists()
  402:     ? studentSnapshot.data()
  403:     : {};
  404: 
  405:   const percentage =
  406:     totalTests > 0
  407:       ? Math.round(
  408:           (Math.max(0, passedCount) / totalTests) * 100,
  409:         )
  410:       : 0;
  411: 
  412:   await setDoc(
  413:     progressReference,
  414:     {
  415:       assignmentId,
  416:       studentId,
  417:       studentName: String(
  418:         studentData.name ??
  419:           studentData.displayName ??
  420:           studentData.fullName ??
  421:           studentData.email ??
  422:           "Student",
  423:       ),
  424:       programmingChallengeId: assignment.challengeId,
  425:       programmingCode: code,
  426:       programmingAttempts: previousAttempts + 1,
  427:       programmingPassedCount: passedCount,
  428:       programmingTotalTests: totalTests,
  429:       programmingPercentage: percentage,
  430:       programmingLastPassed: passed,
  431:       programmingLastError: error,
  432:       updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  433:     },
  434:     { merge: true },
  435:   );
  436: 
  437:   if (passed) {
  438:     await completeStudentAssignment(
  439:       assignmentId,
  440:       studentId,
  441:     );
  442:   } else {
  443:     await startStudentAssignment(
  444:       assignmentId,
  445:       studentId,
  446:     );
  447:   }
  448: }
  449: 
  450: export async function getProgrammingAssignmentResults(
  451:   assignmentId: string,
  452: ): Promise<ProgrammingAssignmentResultsSummary | null> {
  453:   const assignment =
  454:     await getProgrammingAssignmentById(assignmentId);
  455: 
  456:   if (!assignment) return null;
  457: 
  458:   const progressSnapshot = await getDocs(
  459:     query(
  460:       collection(db, "assignmentProgress"),
  461:       where("assignmentId", "==", assignmentId),
  462:     ),
  463:   );
  464: 
  465:   const progressByStudent = new Map(
  466:     progressSnapshot.docs.map((item) => [
  467:       item.data().studentId,
  468:       item.data(),
  469:     ]),
  470:   );
  471: 
  472:   const results: ProgrammingAssignmentResult[] =
  473:     await Promise.all(
  474:       assignment.studentIds.map(async (studentId) => {
  475:         const studentSnapshot = await getDoc(
  476:           doc(db, "users", studentId),
  477:         );
  478:         const studentData = studentSnapshot.exists()
  479:           ? studentSnapshot.data()
  480:           : {};
  481:         const progress = progressByStudent.get(studentId);
  482: 
  483:         const status =
  484:           progress?.status === "completed"
  485:             ? "completed"
  486:             : progress?.status === "in_progress"
  487:               ? "in_progress"
  488:               : "not_started";
  489: 
  490:         return {
  491:           studentId,
  492:           studentName: String(
  493:             progress?.studentName ??
  494:               studentData.name ??
  495:               studentData.displayName ??
  496:               studentData.fullName ??
  497:               studentData.email ??
  498:               "Student",
  499:           ),
  500:           status,
  501:           attempts:
  502:             typeof progress?.programmingAttempts === "number"
  503:               ? progress.programmingAttempts
  504:               : 0,
  505:           passedCount:
  506:             typeof progress?.programmingPassedCount === "number"
  507:               ? progress.programmingPassedCount
  508:               : 0,
  509:           totalTests:
  510:             typeof progress?.programmingTotalTests === "number"
  511:               ? progress.programmingTotalTests
  512:               : 0,
  513:           percentage:
  514:             typeof progress?.programmingPercentage === "number"
  515:               ? progress.programmingPercentage
  516:               : 0,
  517:           completedAt: toDate(progress?.completedAt),
  518:         };
  519:       }),
  520:     );
  521: 
  522:   const completed = results.filter(
  523:     (result) => result.status === "completed",
  524:   ).length;
  525: 
  526:   const inProgress = results.filter(
  527:     (result) => result.status === "in_progress",
  528:   ).length;
  529: 
  530:   const notStarted =
  531:     results.length - completed - inProgress;
  532: 
  533:   const attempted = results.filter(
  534:     (result) => result.attempts > 0,
  535:   );
  536: 
  537:   const averagePercentage =
  538:     attempted.length > 0
  539:       ? Math.round(
  540:           attempted.reduce(
  541:             (total, result) =>
  542:               total + result.percentage,
  543:             0,
  544:           ) / attempted.length,
  545:         )
  546:       : 0;
  547: 
  548:   return {
  549:     assignment,
  550:     results,
  551:     completed,
  552:     inProgress,
  553:     notStarted,
  554:     completionPercentage:
  555:       results.length > 0
  556:         ? Math.round(
  557:             (completed / results.length) * 100,
  558:           )
  559:         : 0,
  560:     averagePercentage,
  561:   };
  562: }
  563: 
  564: export const PROGRAMMING_ASSIGNMENT_RESOURCE_TYPE =
  565:   PROGRAMMING_RESOURCE_TYPE;

==============================================================================================================
FILE: services\programmingChallengeService.ts
==============================================================================================================
    1: import { allProgrammingChallenges } from "@/data/programming/challenges";
    2: import { getProgrammingCurriculumProfile } from "@/data/programming/programmingCurriculumMap";
    3: import type {
    4:   ProgrammingChallenge,
    5:   ProgrammingChallengeFilter,
    6:   ProgrammingChallengeSelectionContext,
    7:   ProgrammingSkill,
    8: } from "@/types/programming";
    9: 
   10: function matchesFilter(
   11:   challenge: ProgrammingChallenge,
   12:   filter: ProgrammingChallengeFilter,
   13: ): boolean {
   14:   if (challenge.mode !== filter.mode) return false;
   15:   if (challenge.difficulty !== filter.difficulty) return false;
   16:   if (!challenge.qualifications.includes(filter.qualification)) return false;
   17: 
   18:   if (
   19:     filter.examBoard &&
   20:     challenge.examBoards &&
   21:     !challenge.examBoards.includes(filter.examBoard)
   22:   ) {
   23:     return false;
   24:   }
   25: 
   26:   if (
   27:     filter.topicId &&
   28:     !challenge.curriculumTopicIds.includes(filter.topicId) &&
   29:     challenge.topicId !== filter.topicId
   30:   ) {
   31:     return false;
   32:   }
   33: 
   34:   if (filter.skill && !challenge.skills.includes(filter.skill)) {
   35:     return false;
   36:   }
   37: 
   38:   return true;
   39: }
   40: 
   41: export function getProgrammingChallenges(
   42:   filter: ProgrammingChallengeFilter,
   43: ): ProgrammingChallenge[] {
   44:   return allProgrammingChallenges.filter((challenge) =>
   45:     matchesFilter(challenge, filter),
   46:   );
   47: }
   48: 
   49: function weakSkillScore(
   50:   challenge: ProgrammingChallenge,
   51:   preferredWeakSkills: ProgrammingSkill[],
   52: ): number {
   53:   return challenge.skills.reduce(
   54:     (score, skill) =>
   55:       score + (preferredWeakSkills.includes(skill) ? 5 : 0),
   56:     0,
   57:   );
   58: }
   59: 
   60: export function chooseNextProgrammingChallenge(
   61:   context: ProgrammingChallengeSelectionContext,
   62: ): ProgrammingChallenge | null {
   63:   const matches = getProgrammingChallenges(context);
   64:   if (matches.length === 0) return null;
   65: 
   66:   const completed = new Set(context.completedChallengeIds ?? []);
   67:   const recent = new Set(context.recentChallengeIds ?? []);
   68:   const weakSkills = context.preferredWeakSkills ?? [];
   69: 
   70:   const curriculumProfile = getProgrammingCurriculumProfile(
   71:     context.qualification,
   72:     context.examBoard ?? null,
   73:   );
   74: 
   75:   const prioritySkills = curriculumProfile?.prioritySkills ?? [];
   76: 
   77:   return [...matches].sort((a, b) => {
   78:     const aCompleted = completed.has(a.id) ? 1 : 0;
   79:     const bCompleted = completed.has(b.id) ? 1 : 0;
   80:     if (aCompleted !== bCompleted) return aCompleted - bCompleted;
   81: 
   82:     const aRecent = recent.has(a.id) ? 1 : 0;
   83:     const bRecent = recent.has(b.id) ? 1 : 0;
   84:     if (aRecent !== bRecent) return aRecent - bRecent;
   85: 
   86:     const aWeak = weakSkillScore(a, weakSkills);
   87:     const bWeak = weakSkillScore(b, weakSkills);
   88:     if (aWeak !== bWeak) return bWeak - aWeak;
   89: 
   90:     const aPriority = a.skills.filter((skill) =>
   91:       prioritySkills.includes(skill)
   92:     ).length;
   93: 
   94:     const bPriority = b.skills.filter((skill) =>
   95:       prioritySkills.includes(skill)
   96:     ).length;
   97: 
   98:     if (aPriority !== bPriority) return bPriority - aPriority;
   99: 
  100:     return a.title.localeCompare(b.title);
  101:   })[0];
  102: }

    1: import {
    2:   collection,
    3:   doc,
    4:   getDoc,

    9: } from "firebase/firestore";

   11: import { db } from "@/lib/firebase";
   12: 
   13: export type QuizAssignmentStatus = "not_started" | "completed";
   14: export type QuizAssignmentSource = "built-in" | "ai-generated";
   15: 
   16: export type StudentQuizAssignment = {
   17:   id: string;
   18:   teacherId: string;
   19:   classId: string;
   20:   title: string;
   21:   description: string;
   22:   resourceId: string;
   23:   type: "quiz";
   24:   quizSource: QuizAssignmentSource;
   25:   dueDate: Date | null;
   26:   createdAt: Date | null;
   27:   status: string;
   28:   className: string;
   29:   teacherName: string;
   30:   resultStatus: QuizAssignmentStatus;
   31:   score: number;
   32:   totalQuestions: number;
   33:   percentage: number;
   34:   earnedXP: number;
   35:   timeTakenSeconds: number;
   36:   completedAt: Date | null;
   37: };
   38: 
   39: type FirestoreDate = Timestamp | Date | string | null | undefined;
   40: 
   41: function convertDate(value: FirestoreDate): Date | null {
   42:   if (!value) return null;
   43:   if (value instanceof Date) return value;
   44:   if (value instanceof Timestamp) return value.toDate();
   45: 
   46:   if (typeof value === "string") {
   47:     const parsedDate = new Date(value);
   48:     return Number.isNaN(parsedDate.getTime()) ? null : parsedDate;
   49:   }
   50: 
   51:   return null;
   52: }
   53: 
   54: function removeDuplicateIds(values: string[]): string[] {
   55:   return Array.from(
   56:     new Set(values.map((value) => value.trim()).filter(Boolean)),
   57:   );
   58: }
   59: 
   60: function normaliseQuizSource(value: unknown): QuizAssignmentSource {
   61:   return value === "ai-generated" ? "ai-generated" : "built-in";
   62: }
   63: 
   64: async function getStudentClassIds(studentId: string): Promise<string[]> {
   65:   const userSnapshot = await getDoc(doc(db, "users", studentId));
   66: 
   67:   if (!userSnapshot.exists()) return [];
   68: 
   69:   const data = userSnapshot.data();
   70: 
   71:   if (!Array.isArray(data.classIds)) return [];
   72: 
   73:   return removeDuplicateIds(
   74:     data.classIds.filter((value): value is string => typeof value === "string"),
   75:   );
   76: }
   77: 
   78: function splitIntoChunks(values: string[], chunkSize: number): string[][] {
   79:   const chunks: string[][] = [];
   80: 
   81:   for (let index = 0; index < values.length; index += chunkSize) {
   82:     chunks.push(values.slice(index, index + chunkSize));
   83:   }
   84: 
   85:   return chunks;
   86: }
   87: 
   88: export async function getStudentQuizAssignments(
   89:   studentId: string,
   90: ): Promise<StudentQuizAssignment[]> {
   91:   const cleanedStudentId = studentId.trim();
   92: 
   93:   if (!cleanedStudentId) return [];
   94: 
   95:   const classIds = await getStudentClassIds(cleanedStudentId);
   96: 
   97:   if (classIds.length === 0) return [];
   98: 
   99:   const classIdChunks = splitIntoChunks(classIds, 30);
  100: 
  101:   const assignmentSnapshots = await Promise.all(
  102:     classIdChunks.map((chunk) =>
  103:       getDocs(
  104:         query(
  105:           collection(db, "assignments"),
  106:           where("classId", "in", chunk),
  107:           where("type", "==", "quiz"),
  108:         ),
  109:       ),
  110:     ),
  111:   );
  112: 
  113:   const assignmentDocuments = assignmentSnapshots.flatMap(
  114:     (snapshot) => snapshot.docs,
  115:   );
  116: 
  117:   const uniqueAssignmentDocuments = Array.from(
  118:     new Map(
  119:       assignmentDocuments.map((assignmentDocument) => [
  120:         assignmentDocument.id,
  121:         assignmentDocument,
  122:       ]),
  123:     ).values(),
  124:   );
  125: 
  126:   const loadedAssignments = await Promise.all(
  127:     uniqueAssignmentDocuments.map(async (assignmentDocument) => {
  128:       const assignment = assignmentDocument.data();
  129:       const assignmentId = assignmentDocument.id;
  130:       const resultId = `${assignmentId}_${cleanedStudentId}`;
  131:       const resultReference = doc(db, "assignmentResults", resultId);
  132: 
  133:       const classReference =
  134:         typeof assignment.classId === "string" && assignment.classId.trim()
  135:           ? doc(db, "classes", assignment.classId)
  136:           : null;
  137: 
  138:       const [resultSnapshot, classSnapshot] = await Promise.all([
  139:         getDoc(resultReference),
  140:         classReference ? getDoc(classReference) : Promise.resolve(null),
  141:       ]);
  142: 
  143:       const result = resultSnapshot.exists() ? resultSnapshot.data() : null;
  144:       const classData = classSnapshot?.exists() ? classSnapshot.data() : null;
  145:       const completed = result?.status === "completed";
  146: 
  147:       return {
  148:         id: assignmentId,
  149:         teacherId:
  150:           typeof assignment.teacherId === "string" ? assignment.teacherId : "",
  151:         classId:
  152:           typeof assignment.classId === "string" ? assignment.classId : "",
  153:         title:
  154:           typeof assignment.title === "string" && assignment.title.trim()
  155:             ? assignment.title
  156:             : "Untitled Quiz",
  157:         description:
  158:           typeof assignment.description === "string"
  159:             ? assignment.description
  160:             : "",
  161:         resourceId:
  162:           typeof assignment.resourceId === "string"
  163:             ? assignment.resourceId
  164:             : "",
  165:         type: "quiz" as const,
  166:         quizSource: normaliseQuizSource(assignment.quizSource),
  167:         dueDate: convertDate(assignment.dueDate),
  168:         createdAt: convertDate(assignment.createdAt),
  169:         status:
  170:           typeof assignment.status === "string" ? assignment.status : "active",
  171:         className:
  172:           typeof classData?.name === "string" && classData.name.trim()
  173:             ? classData.name
  174:             : "Assigned class",
  175:         teacherName: "Teacher",
  176:         resultStatus: completed ? "completed" : "not_started",
  177:         score: typeof result?.score === "number" ? result.score : 0,
  178:         totalQuestions:
  179:           typeof result?.totalQuestions === "number"
  180:             ? result.totalQuestions
  181:             : 0,
  182:         percentage:
  183:           typeof result?.percentage === "number" ? result.percentage : 0,
  184:         earnedXP: typeof result?.earnedXP === "number" ? result.earnedXP : 0,
  185:         timeTakenSeconds:
  186:           typeof result?.timeTakenSeconds === "number"
  187:             ? result.timeTakenSeconds
  188:             : 0,
  189:         completedAt: convertDate(result?.completedAt),
  190:       } satisfies StudentQuizAssignment;
  191:     }),
  192:   );
  193: 
  194:   return loadedAssignments
  195:     .filter(
  196:       (assignment) =>
  197:         assignment.status !== "cancelled" &&
  198:         Boolean(assignment.resourceId.trim()),
  199:     )
  200:     .sort((first, second) => {
  201:       const firstDueDate = first.dueDate?.getTime() ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
  202:       const secondDueDate =
  203:         second.dueDate?.getTime() ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
  204: 
  205:       if (firstDueDate !== secondDueDate) return firstDueDate - secondDueDate;
  206: 
  207:       const firstCreatedAt = first.createdAt?.getTime() ?? 0;
  208:       const secondCreatedAt = second.createdAt?.getTime() ?? 0;
  209: 
  210:       return secondCreatedAt - firstCreatedAt;
  211:     });
  212: }

  104: function convertTimestamp(value?: Timestamp | null): Date | null {

  108: function convertAssignmentDocument(
  109:   assignmentId: string,
  110:   data: FirestoreResourceAssignment,

  113:     id: assignmentId,

  118:     resourceType: data.resourceType,
  119: 
  120:     teacherId: data.teacherId,
  121:     teacherName: data.teacherName,
  122: 
  123:     classId: data.classId,
  124:     className: data.className,
  125: 
  126:     instructions: data.instructions ?? "",
  127: 
  128:     dueDate: convertTimestamp(data.dueDate),
  129: 
  130:     status: data.status ?? "active",
  131: 
  132:     studentIds: data.studentIds ?? [],
  133:     studentCount: data.studentCount ?? 0,
  134:     completedCount: data.completedCount ?? 0,

  137:     updatedAt: convertTimestamp(data.updatedAt),

  141: function createProgressDocumentId(

  148: function createDefaultStudentProgress(
  149:   assignmentId: string,
  150:   studentId: string,

  152:   return {
  153:     id: createProgressDocumentId(assignmentId, studentId),
  154:

  156:     studentId,
  157:

  159:

  162:     updatedAt: null,

  166: function validateAssignmentInput(input: CreateResourceAssignmentInput): void {
  167:   if (!input.resourceId.trim()) {
  168:     throw new Error("A valid teaching resource is required.");
  169:   }
  170: 
  171:   if (!input.teacherId.trim()) {
  172:     throw new Error("A valid teacher account is required.");
  173:   }
  174: 
  175:   if (!input.classId.trim()) {
  176:     throw new Error("Select a class before assigning the resource.");
  177:   }
  178: 
  179:   if (!input.className.trim()) {
  180:     throw new Error("The selected class does not have a valid name.");
  181:   }
  182: 
  183:   if (
  184:     !(input.dueDate instanceof Date) ||
  185:     Number.isNaN(input.dueDate.getTime())
  186:   ) {
  187:     throw new Error("Select a valid due date.");
  188:   }
  189: 
  190:   const today = new Date();
  191: 
  192:   today.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  193: 
  194:   if (input.dueDate < today) {
  195:     throw new Error("The due date cannot be in the past.");
  196:   }
  197: }
  198: 
  199: export async function createResourceAssignment(
  200:   input: CreateResourceAssignmentInput,
  201: ): Promise<string> {
  202:   validateAssignmentInput(input);
  203: 
  204:   const uniqueStudentIds = Array.from(
  205:     new Set(
  206:       input.studentIds.map((studentId) => studentId.trim()).filter(Boolean),
  207:     ),
  208:   );
  209: 
  210:   const assignmentReference = await addDoc(collection(db, "classAssignments"), {
  211:     resourceId: input.resourceId.trim(),
  212:     resourceTitle: input.resourceTitle.trim(),
  213:     resourceTopic: input.resourceTopic.trim(),
  214:     resourceType: input.resourceType.trim(),
  215: 
  216:     teacherId: input.teacherId.trim(),
  217:     teacherName: input.teacherName?.trim() || "Teacher",
  218: 
  219:     classId: input.classId.trim(),
  220:     className: input.className.trim(),
  221: 
  222:     instructions: input.instructions?.trim() || "",
  223: 
  224:     dueDate: Timestamp.fromDate(input.dueDate),
  225: 
  226:     status: "active",
  227: 
  228:     studentIds: uniqueStudentIds,
  229:     studentCount: uniqueStudentIds.length,
  230:     completedCount: 0,
  231: 
  232:     createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
  233:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  234:   });
  235: 
  236:   return assignmentReference.id;
  237: }

  239: export async function getTeacherAssignments(
  240:   teacherId: string,
  241: ): Promise<ResourceAssignment[]> {
  242:   if (!teacherId.trim()) {
  243:     return [];
  244:   }
  245: 
  246:   const assignmentsQuery = query(
  247:     collection(db, "classAssignments"),
  248:     where("teacherId", "==", teacherId),
  249:     orderBy("createdAt", "desc"),
  250:   );
  251: 
  252:   const snapshot = await getDocs(assignmentsQuery);
  253: 
  254:   return snapshot.docs.map((assignmentDocument) =>
  255:     convertAssignmentDocument(
  256:       assignmentDocument.id,
  257:       assignmentDocument.data() as FirestoreResourceAssignment,
  258:     ),
  259:   );
  260: }
  261: 
  262: export async function getStudentAssignments(
  263:   studentId: string,
  264: ): Promise<StudentAssignmentWithProgress[]> {
  265:   if (!studentId.trim()) {
  266:     return [];
  267:   }
  268: 
  269:   const assignmentsQuery = query(
  270:     collection(db, "classAssignments"),
  271:     where("studentIds", "array-contains", studentId),
  272:     orderBy("dueDate", "asc"),
  273:   );
  274: 
  275:   const snapshot = await getDocs(assignmentsQuery);
  276: 
  277:   const assignments = snapshot.docs.map((assignmentDocument) =>
  278:     convertAssignmentDocument(
  279:       assignmentDocument.id,
  280:       assignmentDocument.data() as FirestoreResourceAssignment,
  281:     ),
  282:   );
  283: 
  284:   return Promise.all(
  285:     assignments.map(async (assignment) => {
  286:       const studentProgress = await getStudentAssignmentProgress(
  287:         assignment.id,
  288:         studentId,
  289:       );
  290: 
  291:       return {
  292:         ...assignment,
  293:         studentProgress,
  294:       };
  295:     }),
  296:   );
  297: }

  299: export async function getAssignmentById(
  300:   assignmentId: string,
  301: ): Promise<ResourceAssignment | null> {
  302:   if (!assignmentId.trim()) {
  303:     return null;
  304:   }
  305: 
  306:   const assignmentReference = doc(db, "classAssignments", assignmentId);
  307: 
  308:   const snapshot = await getDoc(assignmentReference);
  309: 
  310:   if (!snapshot.exists()) {
  311:     return null;
  312:   }
  313: 
  314:   return convertAssignmentDocument(
  315:     snapshot.id,
  316:     snapshot.data() as FirestoreResourceAssignment,
  317:   );
  318: }
  319: 
  320: export async function getStudentAssignmentProgress(
  321:   assignmentId: string,
  322:   studentId: string,
  323: ): Promise<StudentAssignmentProgress> {
  324:   if (!assignmentId.trim() || !studentId.trim()) {
  325:     return createDefaultStudentProgress(assignmentId, studentId);
  326:   }
  327: 
  328:   const progressId = createProgressDocumentId(assignmentId, studentId);
  329: 
  330:   const progressReference = doc(db, "assignmentProgress", progressId);
  331: 
  332:   const snapshot = await getDoc(progressReference);
  333: 
  334:   if (!snapshot.exists()) {
  335:     return createDefaultStudentProgress(assignmentId, studentId);
  336:   }
  337: 
  338:   const data = snapshot.data() as FirestoreStudentAssignmentProgress;
  339: 
  340:   return {
  341:     id: snapshot.id,
  342: 
  343:     assignmentId: data.assignmentId ?? assignmentId,
  344: 
  345:     studentId: data.studentId ?? studentId,
  346: 
  347:     status: data.status ?? "not_started",
  348: 
  349:     startedAt: convertTimestamp(data.startedAt),
  350: 
  351:     completedAt: convertTimestamp(data.completedAt),
  352: 
  353:     updatedAt: convertTimestamp(data.updatedAt),
  354:   };
  355: }
  356: 
  357: export async function startStudentAssignment(
  358:   assignmentId: string,
  359:   studentId: string,
  360: ): Promise<void> {
  361:   if (!assignmentId.trim() || !studentId.trim()) {
  362:     throw new Error("A valid assignment and student are required.");
  363:   }
  364: 
  365:   const progressId = createProgressDocumentId(assignmentId, studentId);
  366: 
  367:   const progressReference = doc(db, "assignmentProgress", progressId);
  368: 
  369:   const existingProgress = await getDoc(progressReference);
  370: 
  371:   if (
  372:     existingProgress.exists() &&
  373:     existingProgress.data().status === "completed"
  374:   ) {
  375:     return;
  376:   }
  377: 
  378:   await setDoc(
  379:     progressReference,
  380:     {
  381:       assignmentId,
  382:       studentId,
  383: 
  384:       status: "in_progress",
  385: 
  386:       startedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  387:       completedAt: null,
  388:       updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  389:     },
  390:     {
  391:       merge: true,
  392:     },
  393:   );
  394: }
  395: 
  396: export async function completeStudentAssignment(
  397:   assignmentId: string,
  398:   studentId: string,
  399: ): Promise<void> {
  400:   if (!assignmentId.trim() || !studentId.trim()) {
  401:     throw new Error("A valid assignment and student are required.");
  402:   }
  403: 
  404:   const assignmentReference = doc(db, "classAssignments", assignmentId);
  405: 
  406:   const progressId = createProgressDocumentId(assignmentId, studentId);
  407: 
  408:   const progressReference = doc(db, "assignmentProgress", progressId);
  409: 
  410:   await runTransaction(db, async (transaction) => {
  411:     const assignmentSnapshot = await transaction.get(assignmentReference);
  412: 
  413:     if (!assignmentSnapshot.exists()) {
  414:       throw new Error("This assignment could not be found.");
  415:     }
  416: 
  417:     const assignmentData =
  418:       assignmentSnapshot.data() as FirestoreResourceAssignment;
  419: 
  420:     if (!assignmentData.studentIds?.includes(studentId)) {
  421:       throw new Error("You are not enrolled in this assignment.");
  422:     }
  423: 
  424:     const progressSnapshot = await transaction.get(progressReference);
  425: 
  426:     const alreadyCompleted =
  427:       progressSnapshot.exists() &&
  428:       progressSnapshot.data().status === "completed";
  429: 
  430:     if (alreadyCompleted) {
  431:       return;
  432:     }
  433: 
  434:     transaction.set(
  435:       progressReference,
  436:       {
  437:         assignmentId,
  438:         studentId,
  439: 
  440:         status: "completed",
  441: 
  442:         startedAt: progressSnapshot.exists()
  443:           ? (progressSnapshot.data().startedAt ?? serverTimestamp())
  444:           : serverTimestamp(),
  445: 
  446:         completedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  447:         updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  448:       },
  449:       {
  450:         merge: true,
  451:       },
  452:     );
  453: 
  454:     const currentCompletedCount = assignmentData.completedCount ?? 0;
  455: 
  456:     const maximumCompletedCount =
  457:       assignmentData.studentCount ?? assignmentData.studentIds.length;
  458: 
  459:     transaction.update(assignmentReference, {
  460:       completedCount: Math.min(
  461:         currentCompletedCount + 1,
  462:         maximumCompletedCount,
  463:       ),
  464: 
  465:       updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  466:     });
  467:   });
  468: }
  469: 
  470: export async function updateAssignmentStatus(
  471:   assignmentId: string,
  472:   status: AssignmentStatus,
  473: ): Promise<void> {
  474:   await updateDoc(doc(db, "classAssignments", assignmentId), {
  475:     status,
  476:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  477:   });
  478: }
  479: 
  480: export async function updateAssignmentDetails(
  481:   assignmentId: string,
  482:   updates: {
  483:     instructions?: string;
  484:     dueDate?: Date;
  485:   },
  486: ): Promise<void> {
  487:   const cleanedUpdates: {
  488:     instructions?: string;
  489:     dueDate?: Timestamp;
  490:     updatedAt: ReturnType<typeof serverTimestamp>;
  491:   } = {
  492:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  493:   };
  494: 
  495:   if (updates.instructions !== undefined) {
  496:     cleanedUpdates.instructions = updates.instructions.trim();
  497:   }
  498: 
  499:   if (updates.dueDate !== undefined) {
  500:     if (Number.isNaN(updates.dueDate.getTime())) {
  501:       throw new Error("Select a valid due date.");
  502:     }
  503: 
  504:     cleanedUpdates.dueDate = Timestamp.fromDate(updates.dueDate);
  505:   }
  506: 
  507:   await updateDoc(doc(db, "classAssignments", assignmentId), cleanedUpdates);
  508: }
  509: export async function getAssignmentProgressForTeacher(
  510:   assignmentId: string,
  511: ): Promise<StudentAssignmentProgress[]> {
  512:   if (!assignmentId.trim()) {
  513:     return [];
  514:   }
  515: 
  516:   const assignment = await getAssignmentById(assignmentId);
  517: 
  518:   if (!assignment) {
  519:     return [];
  520:   }
  521: 
  522:   return Promise.all(
  523:     assignment.studentIds.map((studentId) =>
  524:       getStudentAssignmentProgress(assignmentId, studentId),
  525:     ),
  526:   );
  527: }

   74: type FirestoreDate = Timestamp | Date | string | null | undefined;

   79:   title?: string;

   97: type FirestoreResult = {
   98:   assignmentId?: string;
   99:   studentId?: string;
  100:   teacherId?: string;
  101:   classId?: string;
  102:   assignmentType?: string;
