  118: function convertDate(value: FirestoreDate): Date | null {
  119:   if (!value) return null;
  120:   if (value instanceof Date) return value;
  121:   if (value instanceof Timestamp) return value.toDate();
  122: 
  123:   if (typeof value === "string") {
  124:     const parsed = new Date(value);
  125:     return Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime()) ? null : parsed;
  126:   }
  127: 
  128:   return null;
  129: }
  130: 
  131: function normaliseStatus(value: unknown): QuizAssignmentStatus {
  132:   return value === "closed" || value === "cancelled" ? value : "active";
  133: }
  134: 
  135: function normaliseDeliveryMode(value: unknown): QuizDeliveryMode {
  136:   return value === "assessment" ? "assessment" : "practice";
  137: }
  138: 
  139: function safeNumber(value: unknown): number {
  140:   return typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : 0;
  141: }
  142: 
  143: function normaliseIncident(
  144:   value: FirestoreIntegrityIncident,
  145: ): TeacherQuizIntegrityIncident {
  146:   const allowedTypes = new Set<QuizIntegrityIncidentType>([
  147:     "fullscreen_exit",
  148:     "fullscreen_restored",
  149:     "page_hidden",
  150:     "page_visible",
  151:     "auto_submit",
  152:   ]);
  153: 
  154:   const type =
  155:     typeof value.type === "string" &&
  156:     allowedTypes.has(value.type as QuizIntegrityIncidentType)
  157:       ? (value.type as QuizIntegrityIncidentType)
  158:       : "page_hidden";
  159: 
  160:   return {
  161:     id: typeof value.id === "string" ? value.id : "incident",
  162:     type,
  163:     occurredAt: convertDate(value.occurredAt as FirestoreDate),
  164:     questionNumber:
  165:       typeof value.questionNumber === "number" &&
  166:       Number.isFinite(value.questionNumber)
  167:         ? value.questionNumber
  168:         : null,
  169:     detail: typeof value.detail === "string" ? value.detail : "Integrity event recorded.",
  170:   };
  171: }
  172: 
  173: function calculateCompletionPercentage(completedCount: number, studentCount: number) {
  174:   return studentCount <= 0 ? 0 : Math.round((completedCount / studentCount) * 100);
  175: }
  176: 
  177: function calculateAveragePercentage(results: FirestoreResult[]) {
  178:   if (results.length === 0) return 0;
  179:   return Math.round(
  180:     results.reduce((sum, result) => sum + safeNumber(result.percentage), 0) /
  181:       results.length,
  182:   );
  183: }
  184: 
  185: async function getQuizAssignmentResults(
  186:   assignmentId: string,
  187:   teacherId: string,
  188: ): Promise<FirestoreResult[]> {
  189:   const cleanedAssignmentId = assignmentId.trim();
  190:   const cleanedTeacherId = teacherId.trim();
  191: 
  192:   if (!cleanedAssignmentId || !cleanedTeacherId) return [];
  193: 
  194:   const snapshot = await getDocs(
  195:     query(
  196:       collection(db, "assignmentResults"),
  197:       where("assignmentId", "==", cleanedAssignmentId),
  198:       where("teacherId", "==", cleanedTeacherId),
  199:     ),
  200:   );
  201: 
  202:   return snapshot.docs
  203:     .map((document) => document.data() as FirestoreResult)
  204:     .filter(
  205:       (result) =>
  206:         result.assignmentType === "quiz" &&
  207:         result.status === "completed" &&
  208:         result.teacherId === cleanedTeacherId &&
  209:         result.assignmentId === cleanedAssignmentId,
  210:     );
  211: }
  212: 
  213: async function getClassInformation(classId: string): Promise<{
  214:   className: string;
  215:   studentIds: string[];
  216: }> {
  217:   const cleanedClassId = classId.trim();
  218: 
  219:   if (!cleanedClassId) {
  220:     return { className: "Unknown class", studentIds: [] };
  221:   }
  222: 
  223:   const snapshot = await getDoc(doc(db, "classes", cleanedClassId));
  224: 
  225:   if (!snapshot.exists()) {
  226:     return { className: "Unknown class", studentIds: [] };
  227:   }
  228: 
  229:   const data = snapshot.data();
  230:   const studentIds = Array.isArray(data.studentIds)
  231:     ? data.studentIds.filter(
  232:         (value): value is string => typeof value === "string" && Boolean(value.trim()),
  233:       )
  234:     : [];
  235: 
  236:   return {
  237:     className:
  238:       typeof data.name === "string" && data.name.trim()
  239:         ? data.name
  240:         : "Untitled class",
  241:     studentIds: Array.from(new Set(studentIds.map((id) => id.trim()))),
  242:   };
  243: }
  244: 
  245: export async function getTeacherQuizAssignments(
  246:   teacherId: string,
  247: ): Promise<TeacherQuizAssignmentSummary[]> {
  248:   const cleanedTeacherId = teacherId.trim();
  249:   if (!cleanedTeacherId) return [];
  250: 
  251:   const assignmentsSnapshot = await getDocs(
  252:     query(collection(db, "assignments"), where("teacherId", "==", cleanedTeacherId)),
  253:   );
  254: 
  255:   const quizDocuments = assignmentsSnapshot.docs.filter((document) => {
  256:     const data = document.data();
  257:     return data.type === "quiz" && data.teacherId === cleanedTeacherId;
  258:   });
  259: 
  260:   const summaries = await Promise.all(
  261:     quizDocuments.map(async (assignmentDocument) => {
  262:       const assignment = assignmentDocument.data() as FirestoreAssignment;
  263:       const classId = assignment.classId || "";
  264:       const [classInformation, results] = await Promise.all([
  265:         getClassInformation(classId),
  266:         getQuizAssignmentResults(assignmentDocument.id, cleanedTeacherId),
  267:       ]);
  268: 
  269:       const completedCount = new Set(
  270:         results.map((result) => result.studentId || "").filter(Boolean),
  271:       ).size;
  272: 
  273:       return {
  274:         id: assignmentDocument.id,
  275:         teacherId: assignment.teacherId || "",
  276:         classId,
  277:         className: classInformation.className,
  278:         title: assignment.title || "Untitled Quiz",
  279:         description: assignment.description || "",
  280:         resourceId: assignment.resourceId || "",
  281:         dueDate: convertDate(assignment.dueDate),
  282:         createdAt: convertDate(assignment.createdAt),
  283:         status: normaliseStatus(assignment.status),
  284:         deliveryMode: normaliseDeliveryMode(assignment.deliveryMode),
  285:         studentCount: classInformation.studentIds.length,
  286:         completedCount,
  287:         completionPercentage: calculateCompletionPercentage(
  288:           completedCount,
  289:           classInformation.studentIds.length,
  290:         ),
  291:         averagePercentage: calculateAveragePercentage(results),
  292:         integrityTerminatedCount: results.filter(
  293:           (result) => result.integrityTerminated === true,
  294:         ).length,
  295:       } satisfies TeacherQuizAssignmentSummary;
  296:     }),
  297:   );
  298: 
  299:   return summaries.sort(
  300:     (a, b) => (b.createdAt?.getTime() ?? 0) - (a.createdAt?.getTime() ?? 0),
  301:   );
  302: }
  303: 
  304: export async function getTeacherQuizAssignmentDetail(
  305:   assignmentId: string,
  306:   teacherId: string,
  307: ): Promise<TeacherQuizAssignmentDetail | null> {
  308:   const cleanedAssignmentId = assignmentId.trim();
  309:   const cleanedTeacherId = teacherId.trim();
  310: 
  311:   if (!cleanedAssignmentId || !cleanedTeacherId) return null;
  312: 
  313:   const assignmentSnapshot = await getDoc(
  314:     doc(db, "assignments", cleanedAssignmentId),
  315:   );
  316: 
  317:   if (!assignmentSnapshot.exists()) return null;
  318: 
  319:   const assignment = assignmentSnapshot.data() as FirestoreAssignment;
  320: 
  321:   if (assignment.type !== "quiz" || assignment.teacherId !== cleanedTeacherId) {
  322:     return null;
  323:   }
  324: 
  325:   const classId = assignment.classId || "";
  326:   const [classInformation, results] = await Promise.all([
  327:     getClassInformation(classId),
  328:     getQuizAssignmentResults(cleanedAssignmentId, cleanedTeacherId),
  329:   ]);
  330: 
  331:   const resultByStudentId = new Map<string, FirestoreResult>();
  332:   results.forEach((result) => {
  333:     if (result.studentId) resultByStudentId.set(result.studentId, result);
  334:   });
  335: 
  336:   const students = await Promise.all(
  337:     classInformation.studentIds.map(async (studentId) => {
  338:       const profileSnapshot = await getDoc(doc(db, "users", studentId));
  339:       const profile = profileSnapshot.exists() ? profileSnapshot.data() : null;
  340:       const result = resultByStudentId.get(studentId);
  341: 
  342:       return {
  343:         studentId,
  344:         studentName:
  345:           typeof profile?.name === "string" && profile.name.trim()
  346:             ? profile.name
  347:             : "Student",
  348:         studentEmail:
  349:           typeof profile?.email === "string" && profile.email.trim()
  350:             ? profile.email
  351:             : "No email available",
  352:         status: result ? "completed" : "not_started",
  353:         score: safeNumber(result?.score),
  354:         totalQuestions: safeNumber(result?.totalQuestions),
  355:         percentage: safeNumber(result?.percentage),
  356: 
  357:         /*
  358:          * Integrity-terminated assessments never surface XP in the markbook.
  359:          * This also corrects older records created before the zero-XP rule
  360:          * was enforced at write time.
  361:          */
  362:         earnedXP:
  363:           result?.integrityTerminated === true
  364:             ? 0
  365:             : safeNumber(result?.earnedXP),
  366: 
  367:         timeTakenSeconds: safeNumber(result?.timeTakenSeconds),
  368:         completedAt: convertDate(result?.completedAt),
  369:         deliveryMode: normaliseDeliveryMode(result?.deliveryMode ?? assignment.deliveryMode),
  370:         integritySessionStartedAt: convertDate(result?.integritySessionStartedAt),
  371:         integrityIncidents: Array.isArray(result?.integrityIncidents)
  372:           ? result.integrityIncidents.map(normaliseIncident)
  373:           : [],
  374:         integrityTerminated: result?.integrityTerminated === true,
  375:         integrityTerminationReason:
  376:           typeof result?.integrityTerminationReason === "string"
  377:             ? result.integrityTerminationReason
  378:             : "",
  379:       } satisfies TeacherQuizStudentResult;
  380:     }),
  381:   );
  382: 
  383:   students.sort((a, b) => a.studentName.localeCompare(b.studentName));
  384: 
  385:   const completedCount = students.filter((student) => student.status === "completed").length;
  386:   const studentCount = students.length;
  387: 
  388:   return {
  389:     assignment: {
  390:       id: assignmentSnapshot.id,
  391:       teacherId: assignment.teacherId || "",
  392:       classId,
  393:       className: classInformation.className,
  394:       title: assignment.title || "Untitled Quiz",
  395:       description: assignment.description || "",
  396:       resourceId: assignment.resourceId || "",
  397:       dueDate: convertDate(assignment.dueDate),
  398:       createdAt: convertDate(assignment.createdAt),
  399:       status: normaliseStatus(assignment.status),
  400:       deliveryMode: normaliseDeliveryMode(assignment.deliveryMode),
  401:       studentCount,
  402:       completedCount,
  403:       completionPercentage: calculateCompletionPercentage(completedCount, studentCount),
  404:       averagePercentage: calculateAveragePercentage(results),
  405:       integrityTerminatedCount: students.filter((student) => student.integrityTerminated).length,
  406:     },
  407:     students,
  408:   };
  409: }

   87: function convertQuiz(
   88:   assignment: StudentQuizAssignment,
   89: ): UnifiedAssignment {
   90:   const isAIQuiz = assignment.quizSource === "ai-generated";
   91: 
   92:   return {
   93:     kind: "quiz",
   94:     id: assignment.id,
   95:     title: assignment.title,
   96:     topic: isAIQuiz ? "Assigned AI quiz" : "Assigned quiz",
   97:     description: assignment.description,
   98:     resourceType: isAIQuiz ? "AI Quiz" : "Quiz",
   99:     resourceId: assignment.resourceId,
  100:     className: assignment.className,
  101:     teacherName: assignment.teacherName,
  102:     dueDate: assignment.dueDate,
  103:     createdAt: assignment.createdAt,
  104:     status: assignment.resultStatus,
  105:     completedAt: assignment.completedAt,
  106:     percentage: assignment.percentage,
  107:     score: assignment.score,
  108:     totalQuestions: assignment.totalQuestions,
  109:     earnedXP: assignment.earnedXP,
  110:     questionCount: null,
  111:     totalMarks: null,
  112:     submittedAt: null,
  113:     markedAt: null,
  114:     overallFeedback: "",
  115:   };
  116: }
  117: 
  118: function convertExam(
  119:   assignment: ExamAssignment,
  120:   submission: ExamSubmission | null,
  121: ): UnifiedAssignment {
  122:   return {
  123:     kind: "exam",
  124:     id: assignment.id,
  125:     title: assignment.title,
  126:     topic:
  127:       assignment.questionSetSnapshot.topic ||
  128:       assignment.questionSetTitle,
  129:     description: assignment.instructions || "",
  130:     resourceType: "Written Exam",
  131:     resourceId: assignment.questionSetId,
  132:     className: assignment.className,
  133:     teacherName: assignment.teacherName,
  134:     dueDate: assignment.dueDate,
  135:     createdAt: assignment.createdAt,
  136:     status: submission?.status || "not_started",
  137:     completedAt:
  138:       submission?.markedAt ||
  139:       submission?.submittedAt ||
  140:       null,
  141:     percentage:
  142:       submission?.status === "marked"
  143:         ? submission.percentage
  144:         : null,
  145:     score:
  146:       submission?.status === "marked"
  147:         ? submission.totalAwardedMarks
  148:         : null,
  149:     totalQuestions: null,
  150:     earnedXP: null,
  151:     questionCount: assignment.questionCount,
  152:     totalMarks: assignment.totalMarks,
  153:     submittedAt: submission?.submittedAt || null,
  154:     markedAt: submission?.markedAt || null,
  155:     overallFeedback: submission?.overallFeedback || "",
  156:   };
  157: }
  158: 
  159: export function isUnifiedAssignmentComplete(
  160:   assignment: UnifiedAssignment,
  161: ): boolean {
  162:   return assignment.kind === "exam"
  163:     ? assignment.status === "marked"
  164:     : assignment.status === "completed";
  165: }
  166: 
  167: export function isUnifiedAssignmentOverdue(
  168:   assignment: UnifiedAssignment,
  169: ): boolean {
  170:   if (!assignment.dueDate || isUnifiedAssignmentComplete(assignment)) {
  171:     return false;
  172:   }
  173: 
  174:   const dueDate = new Date(assignment.dueDate);
  175:   dueDate.setHours(23, 59, 59, 999);
  176: 
  177:   return dueDate.getTime() < Date.now();
  178: }
  179: 
  180: export async function getUnifiedStudentAssignments(
  181:   studentId: string,
  182: ): Promise<UnifiedAssignment[]> {
  183:   const [resources, quizzes, exams] = await Promise.all([
  184:     getStudentAssignments(studentId),
  185:     getStudentQuizAssignments(studentId),
  186:     getStudentExamAssignments(studentId),
  187:   ]);
  188: 
  189:   const examItems = await Promise.all(
  190:     exams.map(async (assignment) =>
  191:       convertExam(
  192:         assignment,
  193:         await getExamSubmission(
  194:           assignment.id,
  195:           studentId,
  196:         ),
  197:       ),
  198:     ),
  199:   );
  200: 
  201:   return [
  202:     ...resources.map(convertResource),
  203:     ...quizzes.map(convertQuiz),
  204:     ...examItems,
  205:   ].sort((a, b) => {
  206:     const aComplete = isUnifiedAssignmentComplete(a);
  207:     const bComplete = isUnifiedAssignmentComplete(b);
  208: 
  209:     if (aComplete !== bComplete) return aComplete ? 1 : -1;
  210: 
  211:     const aDue = a.dueDate?.getTime() ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
  212:     const bDue = b.dueDate?.getTime() ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
  213: 
  214:     if (aDue !== bDue) return aDue - bDue;
  215: 
  216:     return (
  217:       (b.createdAt?.getTime() ?? 0) -
  218:       (a.createdAt?.getTime() ?? 0)
  219:     );
  220:   });
  221: }

FILE: services\unifiedTeacherAssignmentService.ts

    1: import {
    2:   doc,
    3:   serverTimestamp,
    4:   updateDoc,
    5: } from "firebase/firestore";
    6: 
    7: import { db } from "@/lib/firebase";
    8: 
    9: import {
   10:   getTeacherAssignments,
   11:   type AssignmentStatus,
   12:   type ResourceAssignment,
   13: } from "@/services/resourceAssignmentService";
   14: 
   15: import {
   16:   getTeacherQuizAssignments,
   17:   type QuizAssignmentStatus,
   18:   type TeacherQuizAssignmentSummary,
   19: } from "@/services/teacherQuizAssignmentService";
   20: 
   21: import { getTeacherExamAssignments } from "@/services/examAssignmentService";
   22: import { getTeacherClasses } from "@/services/classService";
   23: import { getAssignmentSubmissions } from "@/services/examSubmissionService";
   24: 
   25: import type {
   26:   ExamAssignment,
   27:   ExamAssignmentStatus,
   28:   ExamSubmission,
   29: } from "@/types/examAssignment";
   30: 
   31: export type UnifiedTeacherAssignmentKind =
   32:   | "resource"
   33:   | "programming"
   34:   | "quiz"
   35:   | "exam";
   36: 
   37: export type UnifiedTeacherAssignmentStatus =
   38:   | "active"
   39:   | "closed"
   40:   | "cancelled";
   41: 
   42: export type UnifiedTeacherAssignment = {
   43:   key: string;
   44:   kind: UnifiedTeacherAssignmentKind;
   45:   id: string;
   46:   title: string;
   47:   topic: string;
   48:   description: string;
   49:   classId: string;
   50:   className: string;
   51:   dueDate: Date | null;
   52:   createdAt: Date | null;
   53:   status: UnifiedTeacherAssignmentStatus;
   54:   studentCount: number;
   55:   completedCount: number;
   56:   completionPercentage: number;
   57:   awaitingMarkingCount: number;
   58:   averagePercentage: number | null;
   59:   detailHref: string;
   60: };
   61: 
   62: export type UnifiedTeacherAssignmentSummary = {
   63:   assignments: UnifiedTeacherAssignment[];
   64:   totalAssignments: number;
   65:   activeAssignments: number;
   66:   overdueAssignments: number;
   67:   completedStudentCount: number;
   68:   totalStudentCount: number;
   69:   awaitingMarkingCount: number;
   70: };
   71: 
   72: function safePercentage(completed: number, total: number): number {
   73:   if (total <= 0) return 0;
   74:   return Math.round((completed / total) * 100);
   75: }
   76: 
   77: function normaliseStatus(
   78:   value: AssignmentStatus | QuizAssignmentStatus | ExamAssignmentStatus,
   79: ): UnifiedTeacherAssignmentStatus {
   80:   if (value === "closed") return "closed";
   81:   if (value === "cancelled") return "cancelled";
   82:   return "active";
   83: }
   84: 
   85: function isOverdue(
   86:   assignment: Pick<UnifiedTeacherAssignment, "dueDate" | "status">,
   87: ): boolean {
   88:   if (assignment.status !== "active" || !assignment.dueDate) return false;
   89: 
   90:   const due = new Date(assignment.dueDate);
   91:   due.setHours(23, 59, 59, 999);
   92:   return due.getTime() < Date.now();
   93: }
   94: 
   95: function convertResourceAssignment(
   96:   assignment: ResourceAssignment,
   97: ): UnifiedTeacherAssignment {
   98:   const programming = assignment.resourceType === "programming-challenge";
   99: 
  100:   return {
  101:     key: `${programming ? "programming" : "resource"}-${assignment.id}`,
  102:     kind: programming ? "programming" : "resource",
  103:     id: assignment.id,
  104:     title: assignment.resourceTitle,
  105:     topic: assignment.resourceTopic,
  106:     description: assignment.instructions,
  107:     classId: assignment.classId,
  108:     className: assignment.className,
  109:     dueDate: assignment.dueDate,
  110:     createdAt: assignment.createdAt,
  111:     status: normaliseStatus(assignment.status),
  112:     studentCount: assignment.studentCount,
  113:     completedCount: assignment.completedCount,
  114:     completionPercentage: safePercentage(
  115:       assignment.completedCount,
  116:       assignment.studentCount,
  117:     ),
  118:     awaitingMarkingCount: 0,
  119:     averagePercentage: null,
  120:     detailHref: programming
  121:       ? `/teacher/programming-assignments/${assignment.id}`
  122:       : `/teacher/assignments/${assignment.id}`,
  123:   };
  124: }
  125: 
  126: function convertQuizAssignment(
  127:   assignment: TeacherQuizAssignmentSummary,
  128: ): UnifiedTeacherAssignment {
  129:   return {
  130:     key: `quiz-${assignment.id}`,
  131:     kind: "quiz",
  132:     id: assignment.id,
  133:     title: assignment.title,
  134:     topic:
  135:       assignment.deliveryMode === "assessment"
  136:         ? "Assessment quiz"
  137:         : "Practice quiz",
  138:     description: assignment.description,
  139:     classId: assignment.classId,
  140:     className: assignment.className,
  141:     dueDate: assignment.dueDate,
  142:     createdAt: assignment.createdAt,
  143:     status: normaliseStatus(assignment.status),
  144:     studentCount: assignment.studentCount,
  145:     completedCount: assignment.completedCount,
  146:     completionPercentage: assignment.completionPercentage,
  147:     awaitingMarkingCount: 0,
  148:     averagePercentage:
  149:       assignment.completedCount > 0 ? assignment.averagePercentage : null,
  150:     detailHref: `/teacher/quiz-assignments/${assignment.id}`,
  151:   };
  152: }
  153: 
  154: function summariseExam(
  155:   assignment: ExamAssignment,
  156:   submissions: ExamSubmission[],
  157: ): UnifiedTeacherAssignment {
  158:   const marked = submissions.filter((item) => item.status === "marked");
  159:   const awaitingMarking = submissions.filter(
  160:     (item) => item.status === "submitted" || item.status === "marking",
  161:   );
  162: 
  163:   const averagePercentage =
  164:     marked.length > 0
  165:       ? Math.round(
  166:           marked.reduce((total, item) => total + item.percentage, 0) /
  167:             marked.length,
  168:         )
  169:       : null;
  170: 
  171:   const studentCount = assignment.studentIds.length;
  172: 
  173:   return {
  174:     key: `exam-${assignment.id}`,
  175:     kind: "exam",
  176:     id: assignment.id,
  177:     title: assignment.title,
  178:     topic:
  179:       assignment.questionSetSnapshot.topic || assignment.questionSetTitle,
  180:     description: assignment.instructions,
  181:     classId: assignment.classId,
  182:     className: assignment.className,
  183:     dueDate: assignment.dueDate,
  184:     createdAt: assignment.createdAt,
  185:     status: normaliseStatus(assignment.status),
  186:     studentCount,
  187:     completedCount: marked.length,
  188:     completionPercentage: safePercentage(marked.length, studentCount),
  189:     awaitingMarkingCount: awaitingMarking.length,
  190:     averagePercentage,
  191:     detailHref: `/teacher/exam-assignments/${assignment.id}`,
  192:   };
  193: }
  194: 
  195: export async function getUnifiedTeacherAssignments(
  196:   teacherId: string,
  197: ): Promise<UnifiedTeacherAssignmentSummary> {
  198:   const cleanedTeacherId = teacherId.trim();
  199: 
  200:   if (!cleanedTeacherId) {
  201:     return {
  202:       assignments: [],
  203:       totalAssignments: 0,
  204:       activeAssignments: 0,
  205:       overdueAssignments: 0,
  206:       completedStudentCount: 0,
  207:       totalStudentCount: 0,
  208:       awaitingMarkingCount: 0,
  209:     };
  210:   }
  211: 
  212:   const [
  213:     classes,
  214:     resources,
  215:     quizzes,
  216:     exams,
  217:   ] = await Promise.all([
  218:     getTeacherClasses(cleanedTeacherId),
  219:     getTeacherAssignments(cleanedTeacherId),
  220:     getTeacherQuizAssignments(cleanedTeacherId),
  221:     getTeacherExamAssignments(cleanedTeacherId),
  222:   ]);
  223: 
  224:   /*
  225:    * T1G integrity boundary:
  226:    * even though each assignment query is teacher-scoped, only assignments
  227:    * attached to a class owned by the authenticated teacher are admitted to
  228:    * the unified teacher portfolio. This prevents stale/corrupt class links
  229:    * from leaking into dashboard and reporting totals.
  230:    */
  231:   const ownedClassIds =
  232:     new Set(
  233:       classes.map(
  234:         (item) => item.id,
  235:       ),
  236:     );
  237: 
  238:   const examAssignments = await Promise.all(
  239:     exams.map(async (assignment) => {
  240:       const submissions = await getAssignmentSubmissions(
  241:         assignment.id,
  242:         cleanedTeacherId,
  243:       );
  244: 
  245:       return summariseExam(assignment, submissions);
  246:     }),
  247:   );
  248: 
  249:   const assignments: UnifiedTeacherAssignment[] = [
  250:     ...resources.map(convertResourceAssignment),
  251:     ...quizzes.map(convertQuizAssignment),
  252:     ...examAssignments,
  253:   ]
  254:     .filter(
  255:       (assignment) =>
  256:         ownedClassIds.has(
  257:           assignment.classId,
  258:         ),
  259:     )
  260:     .sort(
  261:     (first, second) =>
  262:       (second.createdAt?.getTime() ?? 0) -
  263:       (first.createdAt?.getTime() ?? 0),
  264:   );
  265: 
  266:   return {
  267:     assignments,
  268:     totalAssignments: assignments.length,
  269:     activeAssignments: assignments.filter((item) => item.status === "active")
  270:       .length,
  271:     overdueAssignments: assignments.filter(isOverdue).length,
  272:     completedStudentCount: assignments.reduce(
  273:       (total, item) => total + item.completedCount,
  274:       0,
  275:     ),
  276:     totalStudentCount: assignments.reduce(
  277:       (total, item) => total + item.studentCount,
  278:       0,
  279:     ),
  280:     awaitingMarkingCount: assignments.reduce(
  281:       (total, item) => total + item.awaitingMarkingCount,
  282:       0,
  283:     ),
  284:   };
  285: }
  286: 
  287: export function isUnifiedTeacherAssignmentOverdue(
  288:   assignment: UnifiedTeacherAssignment,
  289: ): boolean {
  290:   return isOverdue(assignment);
  291: }
  292: 
  293: export async function updateUnifiedTeacherAssignmentStatus(
  294:   assignment: Pick<UnifiedTeacherAssignment, "id" | "kind">,
  295:   status: UnifiedTeacherAssignmentStatus,
  296: ): Promise<void> {
  297:   const collectionName =
  298:     assignment.kind === "quiz"
  299:       ? "assignments"
  300:       : assignment.kind === "exam"
  301:         ? "examAssignments"
  302:         : "classAssignments";
  303: 
  304:   await updateDoc(doc(db, collectionName, assignment.id), {
  305:     status,
  306:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  307:   });
  308: }
